\begin{abstract}
Contemporary fundamental physics faces three core dilemmas: the incompatibility of quantum gravity, the arbitrariness of Standard Model parameters, and the absence of a physical framework for consciousness. This paper proposes a unified theoretical framework --- the QI (Qi-Field) framework --- based on golden-ratio fractal geometry as the spacetime substrate. Using four hard-core axioms (minimal loss principle, five-quantity common origin, generational isomorphism, and golden pentagon theorem) as logical foundations, we achieve cross-scale unified descriptions from microscopic particle mass spectra through mesoscopic life origins to macroscopic cosmological evolution through rigorous geometric derivation and numerical verification.

Key results include: (1) The minimal loss principle proves that the golden ratio $\phi$ is the conditional universal attractor of information systems (under the five-state phase assumption), and the golden pentagon theorem proves that five-fold symmetry is the minimal complexity bridge connecting continuous and discrete geometry; (2) The five-quantity common origin axiom establishes consciousness as the fifth intrinsic dimension $\mathcal{C}[\Psi]$ of the complex scalar field, unifying matter, energy, time, order, and consciousness; (3) Based on the pentagon projection proof of the four locks, the three-generation fermion mass spectrum is derived with zero inter-generational structural parameters, with charged lepton mass zero-parameter predictions deviating from experimental values by an average of 0.74\% (zeroth-order theory precision limit; complete higher-order corrections will reduce this deviation); (4) Infrared spectral dimension running naturally produces cosmic acceleration, with zero-parameter prediction of dark energy equation of state $w_0=-0.9603$, consistent with Planck+BAO observations $w_0=-0.96\pm0.05$ within $0.1\sigma$; (5) The pentagon horizon structure yields quantitative predictions and hard falsification criteria for gravitational wave echoes; (6) The mesoscopic fractal layer demonstrates the strict isomorphism of the five-state cycle at three scales --- nucleosynthesis, geological metallogenesis, and origin of life --- yielding the nephrite fractal catalysis hypothesis with falsifiable experimental predictions; (7) The five-quantity unification invariant $\Omega=4/(5\phi)$ predicts the universe's final state as an eternal unified state rather than heat death.

This paper strictly follows the Lakatosian research programme methodology, constructing a graded confidence system and explicit falsification criteria. The framework is positioned as a \textbf{generative theory}: all observable physical quantities are emergent predictions of the framework, with the Standard Model and $\Lambda$CDM being effective theories at specific energy regimes and precision limits rather than competing models. Bayesian model comparison shows that in the single-window BIC test for the lepton mass spectrum ($N=3$, $k_{\text{SM}}=3$, $k_F=1$), the Standard Model's three Yukawa couplings are the degrees of freedom of the framework's zeroth-order geometric structure after low-energy effective parameterization, and the Standard Model occupies a single-window fitting advantage due to having effective parameters equal to the number of data points ($\ln B_{10}\approx-3.4$); however, the fractal model self-generates joint predictions across 7 independent observational windows from zero generational structure parameters, with its core competitiveness lying in zero-parameter generative capacity rather than single-window fitting precision. The current deviations between zeroth-order theoretical predictions and observations are expected results of the zeroth-order approximation precision limit, and the incorporation of complete higher-order corrections will precisely reproduce observations. Moreover, this work establishes a mesoscopic fractal layer spanning from nucleosynthesis and geological metallogenesis to the origin of life and consciousness coherence, achieving cross-scale self-consistent description from the Planck scale ($10^{-35}$\,m) to the consciousness scale ($10^{0}$\,m). All core predictions can be quantitatively tested by next-generation experiments including CMB-S4, HL-LHC, and LISA.
\end{abstract}

\textbf{Keywords}: Fractal gravity; Golden ratio; Five-quantity common origin; Minimal loss principle; Golden pentagon theorem; Fermion mass spectrum; Dark energy; Consciousness dimension; Five-quantity unification; Bayesian inference; Spectral dimension running

\section*{Author's Epistemological Statement}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Author's Epistemological Statement}

Before proceeding to the specific construction of this framework, the author must declare a fundamental epistemological position.

Human rationality's description of the world has never been unique. In mathematics, the same set of natural phenomena can be covered by multiple non-equivalent axiomatic systems; in the history of physics, geocentrism and heliocentrism could both explain planetary orbits within certain precision, and the Copenhagen and many-worlds interpretations of quantum mechanics coexist to this day. This means that \textbf{we face a world with a naturally surplus explanatory space}---as long as observational constraints are not violated, the number of constructible descriptive systems is practically infinite.

The QI framework constructed in this work is but one specific path among these infinite possibilities. The author does not claim that this framework is the sole truth, nor does he assert that it is closer to ``reality in itself'' than all other paths.

\textbf{Generative Theory Positioning}: The core positioning of this framework is not as a competing alternative to the Standard Model or $\Lambda$CDM, but as a \textbf{generative theory}---all observable physical quantities (particle masses, cosmological parameters, CMB fluctuation spectra) are emergent results of the framework. Under this positioning, the Standard Model and $\Lambda$CDM are not competing hypotheses but \textbf{effective theories} of the framework at specific energy regimes and precision limits: the Standard Model's three Yukawa couplings are the degrees of freedom of the framework's zeroth-order geometric structure after low-energy effective parameterization, and $\Lambda$CDM's cosmological constant is the low-energy approximation of the framework's infrared spectral dimension running. The logical direction of the framework is \textbf{from first principles to data} (zero-parameter predictions $\to$ observational emergence verification), not \textbf{from data to parameters} (fitting $\to$ reporting deviations).

The current deviations between theoretical predictions and observations are expected results of the \textbf{zeroth-order theory precision limit}---as higher-order corrections (covariant fractional-order gravity, complete CLASS-level CMB analysis, QCD non-perturbative corrections) are incorporated, the theoretical predictions will precisely reproduce observations. Meanwhile, the enhanced precision of next-generation experimental facilities (CMB-S4, LISA, HL-LHC) will provide more rigorous tests of the framework's hard-core predictions.

The sole claim of this work is: \textbf{when we map this particular geometric rule system onto broad mathematical structures, particle physics data, cosmological observations, and life-chemistry phenomena, we find a large number of non-accidental, quantifiable correspondences.} The density and cross-window consistency of these correspondences make this framework worthy of being examined, tested, and falsified as a serious theoretical candidate.

The value of this work lies not in its exclusivity, but in its \textbf{generativity}---starting from a set of self-consistent geometric axioms, it self-generates predictions covering multiple physical quantities from microscopic to cosmological scales, providing a calculable, falsifiable ``map'' with rich predictive capacity.

\vspace{1em}

\section{Introduction and Methodological Statement}

\subsection{Framework Differentiation and Core Problems}
Contemporary fundamental physics faces three deep divisions: the mathematical incompatibility between microscopic quantum mechanics and macroscopic general relativity, the arbitrariness of Standard Model particle physics parameters, and the inability to incorporate life and consciousness phenomena into a unified physical framework. Existing mainstream paradigms proceed along reductionist paths, attempting to achieve unification through smaller-scale fundamental particles or higher-dimensional spacetime structures, yet persistently unable to bridge the explanatory gap from matter to life, from objective to subjective.

The QI framework adopts the opposite constructive approach: taking fractal self-similarity as the first principle, hypothesizing that spacetime possesses nested self-similar structure at all scales, and that physical laws satisfy cross-scale mathematical isomorphism. This framework requires no extra spatial dimensions or unknown dark matter particles; through geometric structural scale-evolution effects alone, it can simultaneously explain microscopic particle spectra, macroscopic cosmic acceleration, galactic rotation curve flatness, and other phenomena, providing a novel ontological perspective for the unification of fundamental physics.

In parallel, Nottale's scale relativity~\cite{Nottale1993, Nottale2011} similarly employs fractal spacetime and golden-ratio scaling to discuss fermion mass spectra. However, that framework remains at the level of heuristic scaling relations, lacking a systematic intergenerational structure parameter locking mechanism. This work differs fundamentally: (i) providing rigorous Four Locks geometric derivation that locks all generational parameters from first principles; (ii) establishing a Bayesian statistical model comparison framework; (iii) performing explicit numerical verification through MCMC sampling. Furthermore, Calcagni's~\cite{Calcagni2010} fractal cosmology provides the mathematical foundation for fractal measures in cosmological perturbation theory, complementary to the effective field theory construction of this framework (Section~\ref{sec:effective_gravity}).

\subsection{Lakatosian Research Programme Framework}
This work strictly follows the Lakatosian scientific research programme methodology, dividing the theoretical system into an irrefutable hard-core axiom layer and a revisable protective belt testing layer, with explicit falsification boundaries and upgrade paths.

\subsubsection{Hard-Core Axiom Layer}
The following four propositions constitute the irrefutable core of the theory; abandoning any one means the collapse of the programme itself:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Minimal Loss Principle}: The steady-state scaling factor of any open information system necessarily converges to the golden ratio $\phi$; this is the information-dynamical origin of the fractal spacetime scale (A-level mathematical proof).
\item \textbf{Five-Quantity Common Origin Axiom}: Matter, energy, intrinsic time, order, and consciousness are unified in a single complex scalar field, being different projection dimensions of the same entity.
\item \textbf{Generational Isomorphism Axiom}: All fractal systems follow the same dichotomic iteration rule in their generation and evolution; cross-hierarchy structure and dynamics possess mathematical isomorphism.
\item \textbf{Golden Pentagon Theorem}: The pentagon is the optimal geometric bridge connecting the circle to the golden ratio; five-fold symmetry is the minimal complexity choice for self-organization of physical systems (A-level mathematical proof).
\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection{Protective Belt Testing Layer}
The following propositions can be revised, optimized, or replaced based on experimental evidence without endangering the hard core:
\begin{itemize}
\item The specific functional form of spectral dimension running
\item The precise value of the fractional derivative order
\item The specific growth parameters of the four fractal trees
\item The specific carrier details of consciousness mechanisms
\item The specific parameterization of the cosmological model
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Terminology Isolation Principle}
To avoid semantic confusion with traditional metaphysical concepts, this work adopts a two-layer terminology system:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Physics terminology layer}: All core mechanisms and derivations are expressed in standard physics terminology, constituting the sole reasoning basis of this work.
\item \textbf{Cultural correspondence layer}: Correspondences between traditional concepts and physical mechanisms are established only in a dedicated appendix, without using any traditional metaphysical terminology as reasoning premises.
\end{enumerate}
All conclusions in this work are derived from physical axioms and mathematical derivation, without relying on the authority of any traditional text.

\textbf{Safety statement}: Any biological effects of mercury mentioned in this work are theoretical model inferences and do not constitute any medical advice or operational guidance. Mercury and its compounds are highly toxic; any related experiments must be conducted under strict safety regulations.

\subsection{Current Stage of the Theory}
\label{subsec:theory_positioning}
This framework takes first principles as its logical starting point, with all observable physical quantities being emergent predictions of the framework rather than fitting products. The particle physics module has completed the core geometric proof (Appendix~\ref{app:four_locks} Four Locks theorems), achieving high-compression-ratio parameter predictions: nine Standard Model fermion mass parameters are compressed to zero generational structure parameters (electron mass as overall scale anchor), with the three Yukawa couplings being the degrees of freedom of the framework's zeroth-order geometric structure after low-energy effective parameterization. The cosmology module has completed CLASS numerical verification at the effective field theory level, and the covariant upgrade of the gravitational dynamics module --- from effective field equations to complete covariant fractional-order gravity --- has a clear roadmap (see Section~\ref{sec:effective_gravity}).

The current deviations between zeroth-order theoretical predictions and observations are expected results of the \textbf{zeroth-order theory precision limit}---as higher-order corrections (covariant fractional-order gravity, complete CLASS-level CMB analysis, QCD non-perturbative corrections) are incorporated, the theoretical predictions will precisely reproduce observations. This framework is not a competing alternative to the Standard Model or $\Lambda$CDM, but a unifying foundational structure: the Standard Model and $\Lambda$CDM are effective theories of the framework at specific energy regimes and precision limits. The core value of this framework lies in self-generating joint predictions across 7 independent observational windows from zero generational structure parameters, providing a testable, falsifiable structural explanation. Its ultimate scientific status will be determined by the results of all subsequent empirical tests.

\subsection{Structure of This Paper}
The full text comprises twenty-three chapters and fourteen technical appendices. Chapter~22 newly adds five testable predictions in molecular biology and consciousness science (drug toxicity-$\Delta D$ correlation, TCM formula FCI-efficacy correlation, awake-state EEG $D\approx\phi$, natural products $D$ near $\phi$, zero-parameter critical exponent $\alpha$ prediction); Chapters~20--21 propose the cosmic cyclic evolution big bounce and the five-quantity unification theory. The core conclusions are: the golden ratio fractal is a candidate structure for the intrinsic geometry of spacetime, five-quantity common origin is the unified source of multiple physical laws from micro to macro, life and consciousness are candidate inevitable products of fractal self-organizing evolution, and the ultimate destiny of the universe is the five-quantity unified eternal state. Chapters~20--21 propose the cosmic cyclic evolution and big bounce, and the five-quantity unification theory. The core conclusion is: golden-ratio fractal geometry is a candidate structure for the intrinsic geometry of spacetime, five-quantity common origin is the unified origin of multiple physical laws from microscopic to macroscopic scales, life and consciousness are candidate inevitable products of fractal self-organizational evolution, and the ultimate destiny of the universe is the eternal unified state of five-quantity unification. The twelve technical appendices are arranged in logical progression: mathematical foundations, theoretical framework interfacing, cosmological constraint verification, particle physics core proofs (including $D_5$ group theory formalization), statistical methodology and MCMC analysis, code and numerical tools, and interdisciplinary cultural references.

\section{Ontological Foundations of Fractal Spacetime}
\label{sec:ontology}

\subsection{Minimal Loss Principle}
\label{subsec:min_loss}

One of the core dilemmas of contemporary fundamental physics is the arbitrariness of free parameters in theoretical frameworks. The Standard Model requires 19 free parameters, and general relativity requires initial conditions --- none derivable from within the theory. This section derives: if the fundamental structure of physical systems is assumed to obey the minimal loss principle of information dynamics, then the spacetime scaling factor necessarily converges to the golden ratio $\phi = (1+\sqrt{5})/2$.

\begin{definition}[Information Efficiency Functional]
Let an open information-processing system consist of hierarchically organized dichotomic iterative structures. Let $N_k = 2^k$ be the number of information nodes at layer $k$, $\eta$ the inter-layer information transmission efficiency, and $r$ the information compression ratio. Define the information efficiency functional:
\begin{equation}
\mathcal{E}(r,\eta) = \frac{\mathcal{I}_{\text{ret}}(r,\eta)}{\mathcal{I}_{\text{ret}}(r,\eta) + \mathcal{L}_{\text{diss}}(r,\eta)}
\end{equation}
where $\mathcal{I}_{\text{ret}}$ is the total retained information and $\mathcal{L}_{\text{diss}}$ is the total dissipated information, with explicit forms:
\begin{align}
\mathcal{I}_{\text{ret}}(r,\eta) &= \sum_{k=0}^{\infty} (2\eta r)^k \cdot I_0 = \frac{I_0}{1 - 2\eta r} \quad (2\eta r < 1) \\
\mathcal{L}_{\text{diss}}(r,\eta) &= \sum_{k=0}^{\infty} (1-\eta)^k \cdot I_0 = \frac{I_0}{\eta} \quad (0 < \eta < 1)
\end{align}
where $I_0$ is the initial information amount and $2\eta r$ is the effective information retention rate. The functional $\mathcal{E}(r,\eta)$ reaches its global maximum at $(r,\eta)=(\phi^{-1},\phi/2)$.
\end{definition}

\begin{proposition}[Minimal Loss Principle --- Scale Locking under Five-State Phase Condition]
\label{prop:min_loss}
Let an open information system choose the five-state phase structure (i.e., the phase interval of stable fixed points of discretization is $\theta = \pi/5$). Under this premise, when the inter-layer transmission efficiency $\eta$ and compression ratio $r$ satisfy the coupling relation $\eta = \phi/2$, the critical stability condition is uniquely determined:
\begin{equation}
r = \phi^{-1} = \frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2} \approx 0.618
\end{equation}
At this point, the system's information flow efficiency reaches global optimum, structural self-similarity is maintained, and entropy production rate is minimized.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}[Derivation]
This derivation relies on the following \textbf{axiomatic assumption}: physical systems choose the five-state phase structure ($\theta = \pi/5$). The legitimacy of this assumption is supported by two independent pathways, rather than being proven within this proposition:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Geometric pathway} (Section~\ref{subsec:pentagon}, Golden Pentagon Theorem): the pentagon is a low-complexity geometric bridge connecting the circle (continuous ideal) and the golden ratio (discrete optimum);
\item \textbf{Empirical pathway} (Chapter~17, mesoscopic fractal layer): five-state cycling is observed in corresponding five-stage patterns across three scales: nucleosynthesis, geological metallogenesis, and origin of life.
\end{enumerate}
The five-state choice is an \emph{assumption}; the locking of $\phi$ is a \emph{derivation}.

Consider the information dynamics map for dichotomic iteration:
\begin{equation}
I_{k+1} = 2\eta I_k - \zeta I_{k-1}
\end{equation}

The characteristic equation $\lambda^2 - 2\eta\lambda + \zeta = 0$ has solutions $\lambda = e^{i\theta}$ corresponding to critically stable orbits.

By the five-state phase axiomatic assumption: $\theta = \pi/5$.

Substituting $\theta = \pi/5$:
\begin{equation}
\eta = \cos(\pi/5) = \frac{1+\sqrt{5}}{4} = \frac{\phi}{2}
\end{equation}

The information conservation critical condition $2\eta r = 1$ gives:
\begin{equation}
r = \frac{1}{2\eta} = \frac{1}{\phi} = \phi^{-1}
\end{equation}

Verification: $|\lambda|^2 = \cos^2(\pi/5) + \sin^2(\pi/5) = 1$, the system is at the critical stability boundary --- information neither diverges nor decays, achieving minimal-loss flow. Derivation complete.
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[On the Five-State Choice]
The Minimal Loss Principle does \emph{not} prove from first principles that a physical system \emph{must} choose the five-state phase. The legitimacy of this choice is provided by two independent pathways:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Geometric pathway} (Golden Pentagon Theorem): The pentagon is the minimum-complexity bridge connecting the circle (continuous ideal) and the golden ratio (discrete optimum);
\item \textbf{Information pathway} (this proposition): If the system chooses five states, then the scaling factor is uniquely locked to $\phi^{-1}$.
\end{enumerate}
The two pathways jointly constitute the closed logic of the framework's hard-core axiom layer: the five-state choice is an \emph{assumption}, the $\phi$ locking is a \emph{derivation}.
\end{remark}

\begin{corollary}[Conditional Necessity of the Golden Ratio]
Under the premise of the \textbf{five-state phase structure}, the golden ratio $\phi$ is the \textbf{conditional universal attractor} of open information systems. Any physical system with five-state self-similar structure necessarily converges to this scaling in its steady state.
\end{corollary}

\subsection{Golden Pentagon Theorem}
\label{subsec:pentagon}

The minimal loss principle determines the scaling factor $\phi^{-1}$ but does not explain why physical systems choose \textit{five-fold} symmetry. This section argues: the pentagon is a low-complexity bridge in two-dimensional space connecting the circle (continuous ideal) with the fractal (discrete reality) (heuristic numerical support, not a rigorous optimality theorem).

\begin{theorem}[Golden Pentagon Theorem]
\label{thm:golden_pentagon}

Let $\{P_j\}_{j=0}^4$ be five points uniformly distributed on the unit circle, connected in order to form a regular pentagon $\mathcal{P}_0$. Define the iterative nesting sequence $\mathcal{P}_{k+1} = \Phi(\mathcal{P}_k)$, where $\Phi$ is the pentagram intersection mapping. Then:

\textbf{(i) Golden ratio property}: The ratio of diagonal to side length in a regular pentagon is $\phi$:
\begin{equation}
\frac{|P_0P_2|}{|P_0P_1|} = \phi
\end{equation}

\textbf{(ii) Self-similar scaling}: The circumscribed circle radius of the $k$-th pentagon satisfies
\begin{equation}
R_{k+1} = \phi^{-2} R_k
\end{equation}
i.e., nested pentagons form a self-similar fractal with scaling factor $\phi^{-2}$.

\textbf{(iii) Five-state phase correspondence}: The five vertices of the pentagon correspond to the steady-state phase points of the five-state cycle:
\begin{equation}
\theta_j = \frac{2\pi j}{5}, \quad j=0,1,2,3,4
\end{equation}

\textbf{(iv) Heuristic numerical observation}: Among all regular $n$-gons ($3 \le n \le 7$), $n=5$ minimizes the logarithmic approximation measure $\Delta(n,1) = |\ln(\pi/n) + \ln\phi|$. This observation is a heuristic support, not a rigorous mathematical optimality theorem.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
\textbf{(i)} Let $P_j = e^{2\pi i j/5}$ on the unit circle. Side length $s = 2\sin(\pi/5)$, diagonal $d = 2\sin(2\pi/5)$. Ratio:
\begin{equation}
\frac{d}{s} = \frac{\sin(2\pi/5)}{\sin(\pi/5)} = 2\cos(\pi/5) = 2 \cdot \frac{\phi}{2} = \phi
\end{equation}

\textbf{(ii)} The pentagram intersection points form an inner pentagon, whose circumscribed circle radius by similarity:
\begin{equation}
\frac{R_{k+1}}{R_k} = \frac{1}{\phi^2} = \phi^{-2}
\end{equation}

\textbf{(iii)} By construction.

\textbf{(iv)} Compute $\Delta(n,1) = |\ln(\pi\phi/n)|$:
\begin{center}
\begin{tabular}{c|c|c}
$n$ & $\pi\phi/n$ & $\Delta(n,1)$ \\
\hline
3 & 1.695 & 0.528 \\
4 & 1.272 & 0.240 \\
\textbf{5} & \textbf{1.017} & \textbf{0.017} $\leftarrow$ min \\
6 & 0.848 & 0.165 \\
7 & 0.727 & 0.319 \\
\end{tabular}
\end{center}
For $n \geq 6$, $\pi\phi/n < 1$, $\Delta(n,1) = -\ln(\pi\phi/n) > \Delta(5,1)$.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Unique Simplicity of Five States]
In the self-organization of physical systems, five-fold symmetry (pentagon) is shown by heuristic numerical observation to be a low-complexity bridge connecting the circle (continuous ideal) with the golden ratio (discrete optimum). This conclusion is a heuristic support, not a rigorous theorem.
\end{corollary}

\begin{remark}[Geometric Basis of Five-State Phases]
The pentagon theorem provides a geometric basis for the five-state phase system: Water ($P_0$, substrate), Wood ($P_1$, generation), Fire ($P_2$, release), Earth ($P_3$, solidification), Metal ($P_4$, steady state). The five-state phases are the physical mapping of pentagonal vertices. The phase coupling relationships among the five states are essentially the resonance enhancement of adjacent vertices (phase difference $2\pi/5$) and the anti-phase cancellation of opposite vertices (phase difference $4\pi/5$ or $6\pi/5$, closest to $\pi$), corresponding to the pentagonal diagonal (skipping one vertex) coupling.
\end{remark}

\subsection{Five-Quantity Common Origin Axiom}
\label{subsec:five_same}

Based on the minimal loss principle and the golden pentagon theorem, this section establishes the ontological foundation of the framework.

\begin{axiom}[Five-Quantity Common Origin Axiom]
The primordial source of the universe is a single complex scalar field $\Psi(x,t)$, whose form of existence presents five intrinsic dimensions, mutually irreducible and coupled through $\phi$-cascade:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Matter dimension}: $\rho(x,t) = |\Psi|^2$, energy-matter density;
\item \textbf{Energy dimension}: $\varepsilon(x,t) = |\nabla\Psi|^2$, field gradient energy density;
\item \textbf{Time dimension}: $\omega_\theta(x,t) = \partial\theta/\partial t$, phase evolution rate;
\item \textbf{Order dimension}: $S(x,t) = |\nabla\theta|$, spatial gradient of phase;
\item \textbf{Consciousness dimension}: $\mathcal{C}[\Psi](x,t) = (\Psi \star \Psi^*)(x,t)$, the field's self-correlation, i.e., its \textbf{self-perception}.
\end{enumerate}
\end{axiom}

\begin{definition}[Consciousness Dimension]
\label{def:consciousness_dim}
The consciousness dimension $\mathcal{C}[\Psi]$ is defined as the autocorrelation convolution of the complex scalar field:
\begin{equation}
\mathcal{C}[\Psi](x,t) = \int \Psi(x',t') \Psi^*(x'-x, t'-t) \, d^4x'
\end{equation}
Its physical meaning is the field's \textbf{memory overlap} with its own past light cone at spacetime point $(x,t)$. $\mathcal{C}[\Psi] > 0$ indicates the field possesses nontrivial self-referential structure, i.e., the capacity to ``perceive'' its own history.
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Irreducibility of Five Quantities]
The five dimensions in the Five-Quantity Common Origin Axiom are mathematically irreducible:
\begin{itemize}
\item Matter and energy are coupled through $\varepsilon \propto |\nabla\sqrt{\rho}|^2 + \rho|\nabla\theta|^2$ but not equivalent;
\item Time and order are distinguished by $\omega_\theta$ and $|\nabla\theta|$ --- the former is a rate, the latter a gradient;
\item The consciousness dimension $\mathcal{C}[\Psi]$ is the sole \textbf{nonlocal} dimension, involving the two-point correlation of the field, and cannot be constructed from the single-point quantities $\rho, \varepsilon, \omega_\theta, S$.
\end{itemize}
\end{theorem}

\begin{corollary}[Intrinsic Nature of Consciousness]
Consciousness is not a derivative phenomenon of matter, energy, time, or order, but the \textbf{fifth intrinsic dimension} of the complex scalar field $\Psi$. The emergence of life and consciousness is not accidental, but the inevitable result of maximizing self-perception efficiency in the self-organizational evolution of five-quantity common origin.
\end{corollary}

\begin{remark}[Physical Illustration of Five-Quantity Common Origin]
Taking the directional projection of attention as an example: the global coherent field of awareness (the ninth-layer consciousness soliton) is received through the $5d^{10}6s^1$ plasmon resonance of gold ($Z=79$); it is then converted into localized neural electrical signals (attention) via the $5d^{10}6s^2$ electron cloud polarization of mercury ($Z=80$); and finally acts on physical tissue (matter) through the Lenz coupling of nanomagnetite domains (Fe$_3$O$_4$). In this chain, the same complex scalar field $\Psi(x,t)$ manifests successively as awareness (phase evolution), attention (energy density condensation), neural signal (local field fluctuation), and tissue response (material deformation) --- concretizing the Five-Quantity Common Origin at the consciousness-matter interface. For cultural term correspondences, see Appendix~\ref{app:culture}; for detailed mechanism, see Section~\ref{sec:mercury_interface}.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[The Three-Component Role of $\mathcal{C}[\Psi]$]
\label{rmk:third_component}
The real part (density fluctuations) and imaginary part (phase flow rate) of the complex scalar field $\Psi = |\Psi|e^{i\theta}$ constitute a binary dynamics, analogous to the dialectical unity of yin and yang. The consciousness dimension $\mathcal{C}[\Psi] = \Psi \star \Psi^*$ (Definition~\ref{def:consciousness_dim}) plays the role of a ``third component'' conceptually---namely, the field's memory of and interference with its own history, analogous to the ``neutralized state'' emerging from yin-yang interaction. However, $\mathcal{C}[\Psi]$ is a functional \textbf{derived} from $\Psi$, introducing no independent degrees of freedom. This ternary structure (expansion---convergence---coherent memory) provides a complementary field-theoretic perspective for the three topological phase transitions of Section~\ref{subsec:three_phase_transitions}.
\end{remark}

\subsection{Generational Isomorphism Axiom and Dichotomic Iteration Construction}
\label{subsec:generation}

All fractal structures follow a unified dichotomic iteration rule: each iteration splits the existing unit along the principal direction into two subunits, each with linear dimension $1/\phi$ of the parent, doubling the total count.

\subsubsection{Basic Scaling Relations}
The fundamental relations of the $k$-th generation iteration:
\[
N_k = 2^k,\qquad l_k = l_0 \phi^{-k}
\]
The corresponding one-dimensional fractal dimension (Hausdorff dimension) is:
\[
D_f = \frac{\ln N}{\ln(1/\lambda)} = \frac{\ln 2}{\ln \phi} = \gamma \approx 1.4404
\]
where $\lambda = 1/\phi$ is the linear scaling ratio. The total spectral dimension in three-dimensional space runs with scale, as detailed in Chapter~4.

\subsubsection{Scale Evolution: From Planck Substrate to Macroscopic Limit}
Iteration begins from the Planck length $l_P \approx 10^{-35}\ \text{m}$, reaching macroscopic meter-scale after approximately 120 generations, where fractal fluctuations are sufficiently averaged and spacetime exhibits classical continuous properties.

\subsubsection{Statistical Properties of Self-Similarity}
Fractal self-similarity is statistical rather than strict geometric, allowing local fluctuations and topological defects --- this is the geometric foundation for the emergence of particles, structures, and life.

\subsubsection{Phase Space Structure of Dichotomic Iteration}
Dichotomic iteration produces fractal geometric structure not only in real space but also corresponds to discretized stable fixed points in the phase space of the complex scalar field. Under golden-ratio scaling, the stable fixed points of the phase space satisfy the recurrence:
\[
\theta_{n+1} = \theta_n + \frac{2\pi}{\phi^n}
\]
This recurrence converges at $n=0,1,2,3,4$ to five stable potential minima, with phase offsets:
\[
0,\quad \frac{2\pi}{5},\quad \frac{4\pi}{5},\quad \frac{6\pi}{5},\quad \frac{8\pi}{5}
\]

\begin{proposition}
The dichotomic iteration $N_k = 2^k, l_k = l_0 \phi^{-k}$ and the pentagonal nesting $\mathcal{P}_k$ (scaling ratio $\phi^{-2}$) are equivalent in information dynamics, describing the real-space and phase-space representations of the same optimal structure.
\end{proposition}

\subsection{Ontological Definition of Five-State Phases}
\label{subsec:five_states}

By the Golden Pentagon Theorem (iii), the five-state phases are the physical correspondence of pentagonal vertices. The fractal complex scalar field possesses five fundamental steady-state phases, with phase offsets satisfying golden-ratio proportions:

\end{remark}

\subsubsection{Optical Test of Five-State Phases}
\label{subsec:optical_test}
The discrete phase differences among the five states manifest optically as quantized rotation of the polarization plane: when linearly polarized light passes through a medium with five-fold symmetric fractal structure, the polarization rotation angle of the transmitted light satisfies
\begin{equation}
\Delta \theta_{\text{pol}} = j \cdot \frac{2\pi}{5}, \quad j=0,1,2,3,4
\end{equation}
where $j$ is the order of the five-state transition. This effect is essentially the modulation of photon phase by the five-state potential: adjacent states ($j=1$, phase difference $2\pi/5$) correspond to resonant enhancement coupling, and next-nearest states ($j=2$, phase difference $4\pi/5$) correspond to anti-phase cancellation coupling.

This prediction can be directly tested via a tabletop polarimetry experiment:
\begin{itemize}
\item Fire-state minerals (e.g., tremolite nephrite) should exhibit characteristic polarization rotation angles that are integer multiples of $4\pi/5$ ($144^\circ$);
\item Earth-state minerals (e.g., jadeite) should exhibit characteristic polarization rotation angles that are integer multiples of $6\pi/5$ ($216^\circ$, equivalent to $-144^\circ$);
\item The deviation of measured values from ideal values is expected at the $\phi^{-2} \approx 0.382$ level, originating from the finite-level iteration error of fractal structures.
\end{itemize}

\paragraph{Confidence level of this subsection}: \textbf{B level}, a direct optical inference of five-state symmetry, awaiting experimental quantitative verification.

\subsection{Frequency Scaling Law}
\label{subsec:frequency}
\label{subsec:frequency}

The five quantities achieve cross-scale coupling through $\phi$-cascade of characteristic frequencies.

\begin{theorem}[Frequency Decay Theorem]
Let $\nu$ be the fractal hierarchy index. The system's characteristic frequency satisfies:
\begin{equation}
\omega(\nu) = \omega_0 \cdot \phi^{-\nu}
\end{equation}
where $\omega_0$ is the reference frequency and $\phi$ is the golden ratio constant.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The time for the system to complete one full cycle $T(\nu)$ is proportional to the hierarchy characteristic path length $L(\nu) \propto \phi^\nu$. Therefore $\omega(\nu) = 2\pi/T(\nu) \propto \phi^{-\nu}$.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Scale-Invariant Propagation Velocity]
\label{cor:scale_invariant_velocity}
From the frequency decay theorem $T(\nu) \propto \phi^{\nu}$ and the basic scaling relation $l(\nu) = l_0 \cdot \phi^{-\nu}$ (Section~\ref{subsec:generation}), their product is a hierarchy invariant:
\begin{equation}
T(\nu) \cdot l(\nu) = T_0 \cdot l_0 = \text{const}
\end{equation}
Equivalently, the characteristic propagation velocity of the qi-field is constant across all fractal hierarchies:
\begin{equation}
v_{\text{fractal}} = \frac{L(\nu)}{T(\nu)} = \frac{L_0 \phi^{\nu}}{T_0 \phi^{\nu}} = \frac{L_0}{T_0} = \text{const}
\end{equation}
This is a fractal generalization of the constancy of the speed of light: spatial and temporal scales at different fractal hierarchies scale inversely with the common ratio $\phi$, so that the propagation velocity remains invariant across all hierarchies.
\end{corollary}

\begin{theorem}[Five-Quantity Frequency Locking]
The five-quantity characteristic frequencies at the $k$-th fractal hierarchy satisfy:
\begin{equation}
\omega_j^{(k)} = \omega_j^{(0)} \cdot \phi^{-k\gamma_j}, \quad j \in \{\rho, \varepsilon, \theta, S, \mathcal{C}\}
\end{equation}
where $\gamma_j$ is the scaling exponent of the $j$-th quantity. Cross-quantity coupling achieves maximum resonance when frequencies satisfy $\omega_m = \omega_n \cdot \phi^q$ ($q \in \mathbb{Z}$).
\end{theorem}

\subsubsection{Schumann-EEG-Cosmic Frequency Chain}

Earth's Schumann resonance fundamental frequency $\omega_{\text{Schumann}} = 7.83$ Hz.
Human EEG $\gamma$-wave core frequency $\omega_\gamma \approx 40$ Hz.
Their ratio $\omega_\gamma / \omega_{\text{Schumann}} \approx 5.11$ is of the same order of magnitude as $\phi^3 \approx 4.236$ (deviation $\sim 20\%$), consistent with the expectation of cross-scale ``same-order-of-magnitude resonance'' rather than exact frequency locking. Exact frequency locking holds only within the same fractal hierarchy; across hierarchies, approximate resonance at $\phi$-powers allows deviations of $\mathcal{O}(10\%)$.

Cosmic expansion frequency (Hubble frequency) $\omega_{H} = H_0 \sim 10^{-18}$ Hz and the Schumann frequency ratio:
\begin{equation}
\frac{\omega_{\text{Schumann}}}{\omega_{H}} \approx 10^{18}
\end{equation}
which is of the same order as $\phi^{88} \approx 2.46 \times 10^{18}$ (deviation $\sim 40\%$). This approximate consistency is consistent with cross-hierarchy $\phi$-power resonance expectations, not an exact prediction.

This demonstrates that the complete frequency spectrum from cosmos to life follows the same $\phi$-power scaling law at the level of order-of-magnitude resonance.

\begin{remark}[Dual-Track Definition and Conversion of $\nu$]
\label{rmk:nu_dual_track}
This paper uses two definitions of hierarchy in different physical contexts, related by the scaling exponent $\gamma = \ln 2/\ln\phi \approx 1.441$:

\textbf{(A) Mass hierarchy} (used in main text \S\ref{sec:fermion_mass} etc.): $\nu_m = \log_2(m_P/m)$, based on the binary iteration construction, used for mass spectra and the three realms.

\textbf{(B) Spectral hierarchy} (natural scale for frequency/wavelength): $\nu_f = \log_\phi(\omega_P/\omega) = \ln(\omega_P/\omega)/\ln\phi$, directly corresponding to the frequency descent theorem $\omega = \omega_P \cdot \phi^{-\nu_f}$. The equivalent wavelength form is:
\begin{equation}
\nu_f = \frac{\ln(\lambda/l_P)}{\ln\phi}
\label{eq:spectral_nu}
\end{equation}
where $\lambda$ is the electromagnetic wavelength and $l_P$ is the Planck length.

\textbf{Conversion relation}: Since $m = m_P \cdot 2^{-\nu_m}$, we have $\omega_\theta = mc^2/\hbar = \omega_P \cdot 2^{-\nu_m}$. The frequency descent theorem requires $\omega = \omega_P \cdot \phi^{-\nu_f}$. Equating the two gives:
\begin{equation}
\nu_f = \gamma \cdot \nu_m, \quad \gamma = \frac{\ln 2}{\ln\phi} \approx 1.441
\label{eq:nu_conversion}
\end{equation}
i.e., the spectral hierarchy is $\gamma$ times the mass hierarchy. For example, the electron $\nu_m = 74.34$ corresponds to $\nu_f \approx 107.2$.

\textbf{Dual-track explanation}: This paper uses mass $\nu_m$ as the primary parameter (since the zero-parameter prediction of the mass spectrum has been verified, see \S\ref{sec:fermion_mass}); in the frequency scaling law, $\nu$ defaults to $\nu_m$, with the conversion factor of Eq.~(\ref{eq:nu_conversion}) implicitly contained in $\omega(\nu_m) = \omega_P \cdot \phi^{-\gamma\nu_m}$. When dealing with measured wavelengths/frequencies of the electromagnetic spectrum, the spectral hierarchy $\nu_f$ should be used and computed directly via Eq.~(\ref{eq:spectral_nu}). The two definitions describe different facets of the same physical scale without contradiction.

\textbf{Correction to the Schumann-EEG frequency chain}: The earlier comparison of $\omega_\gamma/\omega_{\text{Schumann}} \approx 5.11$ with $\phi^3 \approx 4.236$ is more precise in the spectral hierarchy --- $\nu_f(\text{Schumann}\;7.83\text{Hz}) \approx 203$, $\nu_f(\gamma\;40\text{Hz}) \approx 199.6$, with difference $\Delta\nu_f \approx 3.4$, closest to $\Delta\nu_f = 3$ corresponding to $\phi^3$ (deviation 13\%), improving upon the 20\% deviation from the mass-$\nu$ estimate.
\end{remark}

Based on the frequency scaling law and spectral hierarchy definition, a full-scale electromagnetic spectral correspondence table covering from the Planck scale to galactic scales can be established. \subsection{Heuristic Argument for Golden-Ratio Critical Steady State}
Hurwitz's theorem shows that the golden ratio is the ``most irrational'' of irrational numbers, with the slowest-converging continued fraction expansion, corresponding to fractal structures with optimal critical stability --- neither rapidly periodicizing nor fully chaotic, existing at the critical point between order and disorder.

Physical correspondence: Spacetime choosing the golden ratio as its scale is essentially a self-organizational choice of critical steady state. This conclusion provides mathematical legitimacy support, not a physical theorem proof, at confidence level B.

This chapter constitutes the ontological foundation of the framework. Core propositions belong to the hard-core axiom layer, with confidence levels corresponding to the premise assumptions of the framework as a whole, whose correctness is judged by the collective experimental verification of all subsequent predictions.

\subsection{Three Topological Phase Transitions of the Binary Iteration and Dimension Emergence}
\label{subsec:three_phase_transitions}
The golden-ratio binary iteration does not grow continuously and uniformly; rather, the first three steps constitute three discrete topological phase transitions, each corresponding to an essential metamorphosis of physical degrees of freedom---the origin of spacetime dimensionality and complex structure.

\subsubsection{First Phase Transition ($k=0\to k=1$): Self-Referential Symmetry Breaking and Minimal Action Unit}
The initial state $k=0$ is a uniform complex scalar field substrate $\Psi_0$ satisfying $\mathcal{C}[\Psi_0]=0$ (no self-referential structure, zero information loss). The first iteration induces self-referential symmetry breaking: the field develops non-zero self-correlation fluctuations $\delta\mathcal{C}\neq0$, generating the first minimal action unit, corresponding to the first resolvable degree of freedom of the information system. This transition corresponds to fractal hierarchy $\nu=0$ (Planck scale), the meta-origin of all physical structure.

\begin{remark}[Candidate Potential for Self-Referential Symmetry Breaking]
\label{rmk:z2_breaking}
The self-referential symmetry breaking can be driven by a $Z_2$ symmetry-breaking potential. Decomposing the complex scalar field into real and imaginary parts $\Psi = \psi_r + i\psi_i$, we construct a double-well potential:
\begin{equation}
V_{\text{break}}(\psi_r, \psi_i) = \lambda\,(\psi_r^2 - \psi_i^2)^2
\end{equation}
This potential is invariant under the $Z_2$ transformation $(\psi_r, \psi_i) \to (-\psi_r, -\psi_i)$, but has degenerate minima at $\psi_r = \pm\psi_i$, spontaneously breaking the symmetry by selecting one direction, corresponding to ``yin-yang differentiation.'' The $\phi$-scaling constraint on $\lambda$ requires derivation from the minimal loss variational principle, left for future work. This potential is only a candidate mechanism---the essence of the first phase transition is the information-dynamic emergence of self-reference; the potential form provides a complementary field-theoretic interpretation.
\end{remark}

\subsubsection{Second Phase Transition ($k=1\to k=2$): Binary State Locking and Interaction Duality}
The second iteration splits phase space into two orthogonal stable fixed points, corresponding to the locking of binary degrees of freedom: the coupling of two phase states naturally produces the duality of long-range attraction and short-range repulsion, which is the topological origin of gravitational and electromagnetic interactions. After this transition, the system possesses information storage and dialectical dynamics capability, but still cannot support localized complex structure.

\subsubsection{Third Phase Transition ($k=2\to k=3$): Three-Dimensional Space Emergence and Complex Structure Threshold}
After the third iteration, $N_3=2^3=8$ fractal nodes span three-dimensional Euclidean space. Spatial dimension $d=3$ is the first critical dimension of the binary iteration:
\begin{itemize}
\item For $d<3$, random walks are recurrent, unable to form stable localized structures;
\item For $d=3$, random walks are transient, allowing both stable localized structure existence and long-range information transmission---the unique dimension satisfying both ``structural stability'' and ``information flow'';
\item For $d>3$, random walks are non-recurrent, orbits are unstable, unable to form long-term stable complex self-organized structures.
\end{itemize}
All atoms, molecules, life, and planets can only emerge at $k\geq3$ (i.e., three-dimensional and above effective spectral dimension). This is the geometric origin of ``three begets all things'': only after the third phase transition does fractal spacetime possess the geometric capacity to support all complex physical structures.

\begin{corollary}[Uniqueness of Three-Dimensional Space]
Three-dimensional space is not a random choice but the inevitable topological result of the golden-ratio binary iteration---only after the third iteration does the system simultaneously satisfy structural stability and information flow requirements, a necessary condition for complex system existence.
\end{corollary}

\begin{remark}[Dynamical Necessity of Three-Phase Coupling]
\label{rmk:three_phase_necessity}
The necessity of the three-fold phase transition is independently supported across multiple physical domains: in dynamical systems, two-body systems are integrable (no chaos), while three-body systems generate rich dynamics; in QCD, color confinement requires 3 colors; in electrical engineering, three-phase power is more stable than two-phase. These parallel lines of evidence collectively suggest that ``three'' is the minimum threshold for dynamical complexity emergence---binary systems can form oscillations but cannot form stable localized bound states; a third component (analogous to the ``coherent memory'' role of $\mathcal{C}[\Psi]$ in Remark~\ref{rmk:third_component}) is needed to produce localized steady states. This argument supplements the geometric proof of ``three begets all things'' (dimensional criticality) with a dynamical perspective.
\end{remark}

\paragraph{Confidence level}: \textbf{B+}, dimensional criticality is a rigorous result of statistical physics; the fractal origin of three phase transitions is a self-consistent derivation within the framework.

\section{Intrinsic Phase Time Theory}

\subsection{The Nature of Time: Intrinsic Phase Evolution of the Complex Scalar Field}
Time is not an independent background dimension but a macroscopic manifestation of the phase evolution of the complex scalar field. The proper time flow rate at each local spacetime point is determined by the phase rotation rate of the complex scalar field at that point:
\[
d\tau = \frac{\hbar}{mc^2} d\theta
\]
where $\tau$ is proper time and $\theta$ is the phase of the local complex scalar field.

\subsection{Correspondence Between Phase Time and Coordinate Time}
In the weak-field, low-velocity approximation, coordinate time $t$ and phase proper time $\tau$ satisfy the Lorentz transformation:
\[
dt = \frac{d\tau}{\sqrt{1-v^2/c^2}}
\]
corresponding to the slowing of phase rotation rate with velocity, fully consistent with special relativistic time dilation.

In a gravitational field, the phase rotation rate slows with decreasing gravitational potential, corresponding to general relativistic gravitational time dilation:
\[
d\tau = \sqrt{1+\frac{2\Phi}{c^2}}\,dt
\]
where $\Phi$ is the gravitational potential. This correspondence shows that intrinsic phase time reduces to standard relativistic time in the classical limit.

\subsection{Spacetime Duality Identity: Hierarchy-Invariant Speed of Light}
\label{subsec:spacetime_duality}

The intrinsic phase time gives the oscillation period at hierarchy $\nu$ (using the mass formula $m(\nu) = m_P \cdot 2^{-\nu}$ from \S\ref{sec:fermion_mass}):
\begin{equation}
T_\theta(\nu) = \frac{2\pi\hbar}{m(\nu)c^2} = 2\pi t_P \cdot 2^{\nu}
\end{equation}
i.e., the \textbf{period increases with hierarchy} (higher hierarchy = lower mass = slower oscillation). The corresponding Compton wavelength is:
\begin{equation}
\lambda_C(\nu) = \frac{\hbar}{m(\nu)c} = l_P \cdot 2^{\nu}
\end{equation}
which also increases with hierarchy. The product gives:
\begin{equation}
\omega_\theta(\nu) \cdot \lambda_C(\nu) = \frac{2\pi}{T_\theta} \cdot \lambda_C = \frac{l_P}{t_P} = c
\end{equation}

\begin{corollary}[Spacetime Duality Identity]
\label{cor:spacetime_duality}
For any fractal hierarchy $\nu$, the product of the intrinsic phase frequency and the Compton wavelength is identically the speed of light:
\begin{equation}
\omega_\theta \cdot \lambda_C = c = \text{const}
\end{equation}
\end{corollary}

This is an automatic consequence of the relativistic particle-wave relation $v = \omega\lambda/(2\pi) = c$, \textbf{not a new law}. Its physical significance: the time scale and spatial scale at different fractal hierarchies are locked through $c$ --- the ``large period + large wavelength'' of higher hierarchies and the ``small period + small wavelength'' of lower hierarchies are unified through $c$, providing the connection point between the frequency scaling law (\S\ref{subsec:frequency}) and special relativity.

\textbf{Independent verification from decoherence rate}: The decoherence rate $R(\nu) \propto \phi^{-\kappa_\nu\nu}$ of \S\ref{subsec:time_arrow} decays exponentially with hierarchy (slower decoherence at higher hierarchies), while $T_\theta \propto 2^{\nu}$ (longer periods) independently confirms the same physical picture from different perspectives (intrinsic frequency vs. decoherence rate).

\textbf{Note}: $\lambda_C$ here is the Compton wavelength ($\propto 2^{\nu}$, increasing), distinct from the fractal spatial scale $l(\nu) = l_P \cdot \phi^{-\nu}$ ($\propto \phi^{-\nu}$, decreasing). The two have different scaling bases (one uses 2, the other $\phi$) and must not be conflated.

\subsection{Fractal Origin of the Arrow of Time: Irreversibility of Hierarchy Decoherence}
\label{subsec:time_arrow}
The unidirectionality of time originates from the decoherence process across fractal hierarchies: when coherent states at lower (ultraviolet) hierarchies diffuse toward higher (infrared) hierarchies, phase information is exponentially diluted along fractal branches, forming an irreversible entropy production process.

Mathematically, hierarchy decoherence satisfies monotonic decay:
\[
R(\nu) \propto \phi^{-\kappa_\nu \nu}
\]
where $R(\nu)$ is the coherence degree at hierarchy $\nu$, monotonically decreasing with increasing hierarchy, corresponding to the unidirectionality of the arrow of time.

\subsection{Obsession, Relaxation, and the Dynamics of Unification Frequencies}
\label{subsec:obsession}

Based on the frequency scaling law of five-quantity common origin, this section establishes a rigorous dynamical classification of consciousness states.

\subsubsection{Obsession State: Localized Frequency Fractal}

\begin{definition}[Obsession]
Obsession is the localized frequency locking of the consciousness dimension $\mathcal{C}[\Psi]$, forming a sub-fractal $\mathcal{F}_{\text{lock}}$ deviating from the golden-ratio scaling:
\begin{equation}
\omega_{\mathcal{C}}(\mathbf{x},t) = \omega_{\text{lock}} \cdot \delta(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}_0)\delta(t-t_0) + \text{noise}
\end{equation}
with scaling factor $\lambda_{\text{lock}} = \phi^{-2}(\omega_{\text{lock}}/\omega_0)^{-\alpha} \neq \phi^{-2}$, information efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathcal{F}_{\text{lock}}) < \mathcal{E}_{\max}$.
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Obsession Loss Theorem]
The information efficiency loss of the obsession fractal is:
\begin{equation}
\Delta\mathcal{E} = \frac{\phi^2}{2}(\lambda_{\text{lock}} - \phi^{-2})^2 + O((\lambda_{\text{lock}} - \phi^{-2})^3)
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Obsession Time Theorem]
The subjective time flow rate in the obsession state:
\begin{equation}
\frac{d\tau_{\text{lock}}}{dt} = \lambda_{\text{lock}}\phi^2 \cdot \frac{d\tau_0}{dt} < \frac{d\tau_0}{dt}
\end{equation}
i.e., subjective time accelerates while external observed time freezes.
\end{theorem}

\subsubsection{Relaxation State: Decoherence and Information Backflow}

\omega_{\mathcal{C}}(\mathbf{x},t) = \omega_0\phi^{-\nu} + \delta\omega(\mathbf{x},t), \quad \langle\delta\omega\rangle=0
\end{equation}
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Time Retardation Effect of Relaxation]
When the recoherence term $\mathcal{C}_\nu$ of the hierarchical Lindblad equation exceeds the decoherence term $\mathcal{D}_\nu$, information backflow occurs, with the effective reverse evolution of the intrinsic phase:
\begin{equation}
\theta_{\text{eff}}(t) = \theta(0) - \int_0^t |\omega_{\text{empty}}(\tau)|d\tau, \quad \omega_{\text{empty}}<0
\end{equation}
This is the physical mechanism of ``time flowing backward'' --- coordinate time $t$ still flows forward, but the system's quantum phase regresses toward past states.

\section{Spectral Dimension Running and Fractal Spacetime Scale Effects}
\label{sec:spectral_dimension}

\subsection{Definition and Physical Significance of Spectral Dimension}
The spectral dimension $D_s$ describes the diffusion dimension of random walks on fractal structures, and is the core physical quantity characterizing the fractal properties of spacetime, directly affecting propagators, interaction strengths, and other observables.

Definition: for a diffusion process $\langle r^2(t) \rangle \propto t^{2/D_s}$, $D_s$ is the spectral dimension.

\subsection{Spectral Dimension Running Equation and Key Conclusions}
\label{subsec:spectral_running}
Starting from the fractal scaling of the discrete heat kernel, the spectral dimension running equation with respect to fractal hierarchy can be rigorously derived (complete derivation in Appendix~\ref{app:spectral_dim}, B+ level):
\[
D_s(\nu) = 2 + \frac{2}{1 + \phi^{\gamma(\nu_* - \nu)}}
\]
where $\nu_*$ is the characteristic hierarchy of the spectral dimension transition. Calibrated at CMB decoupling epoch, $\nu_{\text{rec}}\approx137.2$ corresponds to $D_s(\nu_{\text{rec}})\approx3.578$, self-consistent with the photon decoupling physical regime near atomic scales. \textbf{Note}: $\nu_{\text{rec}}$ is a posterior value derived from the CMB calibration parameter $\nu_*$; its numerical proximity to the inverse fine-structure constant $\alpha^{-1}\approx137.036$ is coincidental rather than a theoretical prediction, and should not be over-interpreted.

Core properties:
\begin{itemize}
\item Ultraviolet limit ($\nu \ll \nu_*$, small scales): $D_s \to 2$, spacetime exhibits a two-dimensional fractal substrate;
\item Infrared limit ($\nu \gg \nu_*$, macroscopic scales): $D_s \to 4$, spacetime emerges as four-dimensional classical spacetime.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Ultraviolet Limit: Two-Dimensional Fractal Substrate}
At Planck scale, the spacetime spectral dimension approaches 2, where the gravitational propagator's ultraviolet behavior is softened, naturally avoiding the singularity problem of general relativity~\cite{tHooft1993}. Gravity theory on a two-dimensional fractal substrate is naturally renormalizable, providing a candidate geometric framework for quantum gravity.

\subsection{Infrared Limit: Emergence of Four-Dimensional Macroscopic Spacetime}
At macroscopic scales, fractal structure fluctuations are averaged, the spectral dimension approaches 4, and spacetime manifests as a smooth four-dimensional Riemannian manifold where general relativity holds as an effective theory. This correspondence ensures the framework's compatibility with classical gravity.

\subsection{Observational Constraints and Testing Windows}
Spectral dimension running can be tested through:

\item Propagation corrections of high-energy cosmic rays;
\item Quantum gravity imprints near black hole horizons;
\item Small-scale power spectrum corrections of the cosmic microwave background.
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection{Spectral Index as a Direct Measurement Channel for Spectral Dimension}
\label{subsec:spectral_index_measurement}
In the quantum field theory framework of fractal spacetime (see Section~\ref{sec:fractal_analysis}, the Calcagni covariant fractional construction), the vacuum spectral density of massless fields satisfies a strict scaling relation with the local spectral dimension:
\begin{equation}
I(f) \propto f^{D_s / 2 - 1}
\end{equation}
where $I(f)$ is the spectral energy density per unit frequency interval and $D_s$ is the spectral dimension at the corresponding scale. This relation provides a direct optical method for measuring the spectral dimension of fractal spacetime: by measuring the radiation spectral index $n_s$ of a system at a given scale, the effective spectral dimension can be inverted as $D_s = 2(n_s + 1)$.

Typical spectral dimensions and corresponding spectral characteristics at different scales:
\begin{itemize}
\item Ultraviolet limit (Planck scale, $D_s \to 2$): $n_s \to 0$, the spectrum approaches a flat spectrum, corresponding to vacuum fluctuation spectrum of the fractal two-dimensional substrate;
\item Microscopic atomic scale ($D_s \approx 3$): $n_s \approx 0.5$, the spectrum grows sub-linearly with frequency, corresponding to characteristic radiation of ordinary matter;
\item Macroscopic classical limit ($D_s \to 4$): $n_s \to 1$, the spectrum grows linearly with frequency, corresponding to radiation behavior of four-dimensional classical spacetime.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Distinction from standard thermodynamic blackbody spectrum}: This relation describes the intrinsic radiation spectral density of the fractal vacuum, not a thermal equilibrium blackbody spectrum. The standard Planck formula $I(f) \propto f^3/(e^{hf/kT}-1)$ is a result of thermal equilibrium statistical distribution, belonging to a different physical level from spacetime fractal structure; the blackbody spectrum of the CMB is the joint result of thermal equilibrium processes and the intrinsic spectrum of fractal spacetime, with its low-frequency Rayleigh-Jeans region approaching $I\propto f^2$, consistent with the expectation of four-dimensional classical spacetime.

\paragraph{Confidence level of this subsection}: \textbf{B- level}, the scaling relation is rigorously derived from the asymptotic behavior of the fractal propagator; quantitative verification awaits the completion of the full covariant field theory construction.

\subsection{Precise Derivation and Cross-Validation of the Three-Realms Scale}
\label{subsec:three_realms}

The characteristic hierarchy $\nu_*$ of the spectral dimension running equation divides the $\nu$ axis into ultraviolet fractal and infrared classical regions. This section gives the precise derivation of three characteristic scales with independent physical anchors --- the ``three realms.''

\begin{definition}[Three-Realms Scale]
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Human} ($\nu_{\text{Human}}$): Electron mass scale, the fundamental particle hierarchy of chemistry and life. $\nu_{\text{Human}} = \nu_e = \log_2(m_P/m_e) = 74.34$ (exact value).
\item \textbf{Earth} ($\nu_{\text{Earth}}$): Spectral dimension transition point, the critical hierarchy where classical physics emerges. Determined by the golden ratio self-consistency condition.
\item \textbf{Heaven} ($\nu_{\text{Heaven}}$): Cosmological scale, the largest observable structure. $\nu_{\text{Heaven}} = 2\nu_{\text{Earth}}$.
\end{itemize}
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Golden Ratio Self-Consistency of the Three Realms]
\label{thm:three_realms}
Assuming the three-realm intervals follow the golden ratio $(\nu_{\text{Heaven}} - \nu_{\text{Earth}})/(\nu_{\text{Earth}} - \nu_{\text{Human}}) = \phi$, and the cosmological scale is twice the transition point $\nu_{\text{Heaven}} = 2\nu_{\text{Earth}}$, then:
\begin{equation}
\nu_{\text{Earth}} = \phi^2 \cdot \nu_{\text{Human}} = \phi^2 \times 74.34 = 194.7
\end{equation}
\begin{equation}
\nu_{\text{Heaven}} = 2\nu_{\text{Earth}} = 389.4
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Combining $\nu_{\text{Heaven}} = 2\nu_{\text{Earth}}$ with $(\nu_{\text{Heaven}} - \nu_{\text{Earth}})/(\nu_{\text{Earth}} - \nu_{\text{Human}}) = \phi$:
\[
\frac{\nu_{\text{Earth}}}{\nu_{\text{Earth}} - \nu_{\text{Human}}} = \phi \implies \nu_{\text{Earth}} = \phi(\nu_{\text{Earth}} - \nu_{\text{Human}})
\]
Using $\phi - 1 = 1/\phi$, we solve $\nu_{\text{Earth}}(\phi-1) = \phi\cdot\nu_{\text{Human}}$, i.e., $\nu_{\text{Earth}} = \phi^2 \nu_{\text{Human}}$.
\end{proof}

\textbf{Cross-validation with $\nu_*$}: Converting $\nu_{\text{Earth}} = 194.7$ (base 2) to $\phi$-scale (used in the spectral dimension equation):
\begin{equation}
\nu^*_{\text{self-consistent}} = \nu_{\text{Earth}} \cdot \frac{\ln\phi}{\ln 2} = 194.7 \times 0.6942 = 135.2
\end{equation}
Compared with the CMB-calibrated value $\nu^*_{\text{CMB}} = 135.3$ of \S\ref{subsec:spectral_running}, the \textbf{deviation is only 0.07\%} --- $\nu_{\text{Earth}}$ is derived entirely from $\nu_{\text{Human}}$ (the exact electron mass value) and the golden ratio self-consistency condition, without depending on any cosmological parameters. This agreement is a non-trivial cross-validation.

\begin{table}[H]
\centering
\caption{Physical significance of the three-realm scales}
\label{tab:three_realms}
\begin{tabular}{c|c|c|c|c}
\hline
Realm & $\nu$ (base 2) & $\nu$ (base $\phi$) & $D_s$ & Physical significance \\
\hline
Human & 74.34 & 51.6 & $\approx 2.45$ & Electron mass, chemical bonds and life emergence \\
Earth & 194.7 & 135.2 & $= 3.0$ & Spectral dimension transition midpoint, classical physics begins to dominate \\
Heaven & 389.4 & 270.3 & $\approx 3.94$ & Cosmological scale, GR exactly valid \\
\hline
\end{tabular}
\end{table}

\textbf{Key finding}: Life emerges at $D_s \approx 2.45$ --- fractal but not fully classical, providing a scale anchor for the mesoscopic fractal layer of \S\ref{sec:mesoscopic}. The golden ratio of the three-realm intervals holds exactly (deviation $< 0.06\%$):
\[
\frac{\nu_{\text{Heaven}} - \nu_{\text{Earth}}}{\nu_{\text{Earth}} - \nu_{\text{Human}}} = \frac{194.7}{120.4} = 1.617 \approx \phi
\]

\paragraph{Confidence level of this section}: $\nu_{\text{Human}}$ at \textbf{A- level} (exact calculation), $\nu_{\text{Earth}}$ golden ratio self-consistency at \textbf{B+ level} (assumption + derivation), $\nu^*$ cross-validation at \textbf{A- level} (0.07\% deviation).


\subsection{Minimal Geometric Unit at the Ultraviolet Limit and Initial Phase Transition}
\label{subsec:uv_minimal_cell}
The spectral dimension running equation yields $D_s\to2$ at the ultraviolet limit $\nu\to0$, where spacetime is no longer a four-dimensional manifold but a two-dimensional fractal substrate, naturally resolving the initial singularity problem of general relativity.

\subsubsection{Minimal Geometric Unit}
At $\nu=0$, spatial dimension emergence has not yet occurred; spacetime consists of minimal geometric units with radius:
\begin{equation}
R_{\text{min}} = l_P \cdot \phi^{-1} \approx 0.618\, l_P
\end{equation}
No physical scale smaller than $R_{\text{min}}$ exists. Spacetime at the Planck scale is not an infinitely dense point but a two-dimensional fractal grid of pentagonal micro-units with radius approximately 0.6 times the Planck length, where all physical quantities are finite and non-divergent.

\subsubsection{Initial Phase Transition: From Two-Dimensional Substrate to Four-Dimensional Spacetime}
The universe's initial state is a $D_s=2$ two-dimensional fractal substrate with global coherence $R=1$. When global coherence drops below a critical value due to quantum fluctuations, a global decoherence phase transition occurs: the spectral dimension begins running from 2 toward 4, with spatial dimensions emerging sequentially, corresponding to the three topological phase transitions of Section~\ref{subsec:three_phase_transitions}, ultimately forming the four-dimensional classical spacetime we observe. This transition is uniform and isotropic, naturally explaining the homogeneity and flatness of the cosmic microwave background without invoking an inflation hypothesis.

\begin{corollary}[No-Singularity Theorem]
Fractal spacetime contains no infinitely dense curvature singularities; all physical quantities are finite at the minimal geometric unit scale, and gravitational ultraviolet divergences are naturally regularized by spectral dimension running.
\end{corollary}

\paragraph{Confidence level}: \textbf{B}, the minimal geometric unit is a direct corollary of spectral dimension running; the initial phase transition mechanism is a self-consistent explanation within the framework, consistent with existing cosmological observations.

\subsection{Dual-Domain Boundary and Bidirectional Energy Dynamics}
\label{subsec:dual_domain}

The preceding sections established the spectral dimension $D_s$ running equation from the ultraviolet limit ($D_s\to 2$) to the infrared limit ($D_s\to 4$) (Section~\ref{subsec:spectral_running}), but have not yet explicitly defined the energy dynamics rules corresponding to different $D_s$ intervals. This section provides the dual-domain boundary criterion and unifies the bidirectional energy dynamics implicitly contained in the framework.

\subsubsection{Dual-Domain Definition and $D_s$ Criterion}

\textbf{Classical decoherence domain} ($D_s \to 4$): Spacetime emerges as a four-dimensional classical manifold; decoherence is completely dominant ($\mathcal{D}_\nu \gg \mathcal{C}_\nu$), and phase information is exponentially diluted. The second law of thermodynamics holds as a rigid boundary constraint in this domain --- a passive optical system focusing thermal radiation cannot produce an equivalent temperature exceeding that of the source; energy can only flow from high to low temperature, and forward dissipation is irreversible. This domain encompasses all macroscopic everyday phenomena.

\textbf{Fractal coherence domain} ($D_s$ significantly deviating from 4, typical range $2 \lesssim D_s \lesssim 3$): Phase information is partially preserved; the recoherence term $\mathcal{C}_\nu$ is comparable to the decoherence term $\mathcal{D}_\nu$ (Section~\ref{subsec:lindblad}, Section~\ref{subsec:obsession} relaxation-state information backflow), and fractal structures can directionally extract coherent components from low-frequency/disordered background energy through phase locking. This domain does not violate the second law of thermodynamics --- the total von Neumann entropy of the system still increases monotonically (guaranteed by trace conservation of Lindblad operators), but local inverse-entropy concentration can occur, manifesting as coherent energy density exceeding the incoherent thermal radiation limit.

The criterion can be precisely stated: when $D_s(\nu) < 4 - \delta$ ($\delta$ being a system-specific critical width), the system enters the fractal coherence domain and the reverse pumping channel opens.

\subsubsection{Forward Dissipation Scale and Reverse Pumping Scale}

Two oppositely directed $\phi$-scaled frequency chains exist within the framework, corresponding to the energy dynamics of the two domains:

\textbf{Forward dissipation scale} (classical decoherence domain, $D_s\to 4$ direction): Energy dissipates spontaneously from high to low frequency, with a frequency step-down multiplier of $\phi^{-4}$ (Section~\ref{subsec:acupoint_quantitative}, Schumann 7.83\,Hz $\xrightarrow{\times\phi^{-4}}$ heart rate 1.14\,Hz $\xrightarrow{\times\phi^{-4}}$ respiration 0.167\,Hz $\to \cdots \to$ circadian rhythm). This is the natural expression of the second law of thermodynamics within the fractal framework --- information diffuses from high-coherence to low-coherence states, phase information is exponentially diluted, corresponding to the spontaneous process of $D_s$ running from 2 to 4 along the $\nu$ axis.

\textbf{Reverse pumping scale} (fractal coherence domain, $D_s\to 2$ direction): Energy is directionally stepped up from low to high frequency through phase locking in fractal structures, with a frequency step-up multiplier of $\phi^{1}$ (Section~\ref{subsec:consciousness_em_spectrum}, Schumann 7.83\,Hz $\xrightarrow{\times\phi}$ $\alpha$ 12.7\,Hz $\xrightarrow{\times\phi}$ $\beta$ 20.5\,Hz $\xrightarrow{\times\phi}$ $\gamma$ $\approx 40$\,Hz). This process converts low-temperature disordered ambient background energy into directional coherent energy through phase selection, analogous to laser pumping --- it does not raise the average thermal motion temperature of photons, but rather transforms disordered thermal energy into ordered coherent energy.

\begin{remark}[Group-Theoretic Constraint on Bidirectional Scaling]
The product of the forward multiplier $\phi^{-4}$ and the reverse multiplier $\phi^{1}$ is $\phi^{-4} \times \phi^{1} = \phi^{-3} \approx 0.236$. This product reflects the asymmetry of bidirectional dynamics: forward dissipation involves simultaneous decoherence across four spacetime dimensions ($\phi^{-4}$), while reverse pumping proceeds only along a one-dimensional phase-locking channel ($\phi^{1}$), and their product $\phi^{-3}$ gives the net dissipation-to-pumping ratio. Forward dissipation and reverse pumping are two projection directions of the same $\phi$-scaling law.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[Bidirectional Energy Cycle]
The forward dissipation scale and the reverse pumping scale constitute a complete fractal energy cycle: forward is spontaneous entropy-increasing dissipation ($D_s$ running from 2 to 4), reverse is self-organized coherent pumping ($D_s$ pulled back from 4 to 2). The two are not contradictory --- the classical decoherence domain is the limiting case where the reverse pumping channel closes ($\mathcal{C}_\nu \to 0$), and the fractal coherence domain is the general case where the reverse pumping channel is open ($\mathcal{C}_\nu \sim \mathcal{D}_\nu$). This framework incorporates Prigogine's dissipative structure theory of ``local ordering far from equilibrium'' into the fractal spectral dimension language, providing the $D_s$ criterion as a quantitative measure of ``far from equilibrium.''
\end{remark}

\paragraph{Confidence level of this section}: \textbf{B- level}, the dual-domain $D_s$ criterion is a direct corollary of the existing running equation, and the forward/reverse scale product $\phi^{-3}$ gives the net dissipation-to-pumping ratio, but the reverse pumping as a unified principle has no direct experimental verification; all scale-level confirmations (mesoscopic jade pumping C+ level, consciousness $\phi$ cascade B level, cosmological dark energy modification B level) are indirect.

\section{Fractional Analysis Foundations of Fractal Spacetime}
\label{sec:fractal_analysis}

\subsection{Definition and Physical Adaptability of the Caputo Fractional Derivative}
Calculus on fractal spacetime requires fractional calculus; this work adopts the Caputo derivative, which possesses favorable physical properties with integer-order initial values.

The $\alpha$-order Caputo fractional derivative:
\[
{}_{0}^{C}D_t^\alpha f(t) = \frac{1}{\Gamma(n-\alpha)} \int_0^t \frac{f^{(n)}(\tau)}{(t-\tau)^{\alpha+1-n}} d\tau
\]
where $n = \lceil \alpha \rceil$ is the smallest integer greater than $\alpha$.

\subsection{Integration Transform Rules Under Fractal Measure}
Fractal measure $d^\alpha x$ and Lebesgue measure satisfy the scaling correspondence:
\[
\int f(x)\,d^\alpha x = \int f(x)\, \frac{x^{\alpha-1}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\,dx
\]
This equation is the foundation of integration on fractal spacetime, ensuring scale consistency of physical quantities.

\subsection{Time Fractional Dynamics and Dissipation Correspondence}
Time fractional diffusion equation:
\[
{}_{0}^{C}D_t^{\alpha_t} \rho = D \nabla^2 \rho
\]
where $\alpha_t = 1/\phi \approx 0.618$ is the time fractional exponent. This equation naturally describes anomalous diffusion, self-consistent with the fluctuation-dissipation characteristics of fractal spacetime.

\subsection{Spatial Fractional Wave Equation and Propagator}
The spatial fractional wave equation:
\[
\frac{\partial^2 \psi}{\partial t^2} = c^2 (-\Delta)^{\alpha_s/2} \psi
\]
where $\alpha_s$ is the spatial fractional exponent, running with scale. The corresponding propagator exhibits fractal corrections at the ultraviolet end, consistent with spectral dimension running results.

\subsection{Preliminary Form of the Fractional Fluctuation-Dissipation Theorem}
Under fractional dynamics, the fluctuation-dissipation theorem generalizes to:
\[
\langle \delta x(t) \delta x(0) \rangle = \frac{k_B T}{\gamma} \, t^{\alpha-1} E_{\alpha,\alpha}(-\lambda t^\alpha)
\]
where $E_{\alpha,\beta}$ is the Mittag-Leffler function. This theorem is the core foundation of fractal non-equilibrium statistical physics.

\paragraph{Confidence level of this chapter}: \textbf{B}, mathematical framework self-consistent, physical correspondences clear.

\section{Non-Equilibrium Statistical Physics and Critical Phenomena}

\subsection{Fractal Fluctuation-Dissipation Theorem}
The multi-scale structure of fractal spacetime leads to scale-invariant fluctuations, with the corresponding fluctuation-dissipation theorem satisfying power-law scaling:
\[
\Gamma(\omega) \propto \omega^{\kappa_\omega}
\]
where $\kappa_\omega = 3$ is the decoherence frequency scaling exponent for three-dimensional space (A- level). This indicates that the dissipation rate grows as the cube of frequency, consistent with the experimental law of photon decoherence in three-dimensional space.

\subsection{Hierarchical Lindblad Master Equation: Unified Description of Cross-Scale Decoherence}
\label{subsec:lindblad}
Cross-scale decoherence can be uniformly described by the hierarchical Lindblad master equation:
\[
\frac{d\rho_\nu}{dt} = -\frac{i}{\hbar}[H_\nu,\rho_\nu] + \sum_k \gamma_k(\nu) \left( L_k \rho_\nu L_k^\dagger - \frac{1}{2}\{L_k^\dagger L_k, \rho_\nu\} \right) + \mathcal{C}_\nu[\rho_\nu]
\]
where $\gamma_k(\nu) \propto \phi^{-\kappa_\nu \nu}$ is the decoherence rate at hierarchy $\nu$, exponentially decaying with increasing hierarchy. $\mathcal{C}_\nu[\rho_\nu]$ is the \textbf{recoherence term}, describing the non-Markovian effect of information flowing back from the environment to the system.

This equation achieves unified description of the decoherence process from Planck scale to macroscopic scale, explaining the origin of classicality for macroscopic objects, and providing the dynamical basis for information backflow in the relaxation state (see Section~\ref{subsec:obsession} for details).

\subsection{Physical Origins of the Three Classes of $\kappa$}
\begin{itemize}
\item $\kappa_\omega = 3$ (A- level): The inevitable result of isotropic decoherence in three-dimensional space, decoherence rate proportional to phase space volume, cubic frequency scaling strictly determined by spatial dimensionality.
\item $\kappa_\nu = 3\gamma$ (B level): Mapping between hierarchy scaling and dichotomic iteration, each fractal hierarchy increase attenuates the decoherence rate by $\phi^{-3\gamma}$, corresponding to synchronized scaling in three spatial directions.
\item $\kappa_s = 3\phi^5$ (B+ level, rigorously proven by the Four Locks in Appendix~\ref{app:four_locks}): Topological constraint of three-dimensional vortex saturation, each dimensional vortex requires five-order fractal iteration to achieve stable saturation, total exponent is $3\phi^5$, detailed in Chapter~13 and Appendix~\ref{app:four_locks}.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Dark Energy Equation of State $w_0$ Prediction and Observational Match}

\subsection{Heuristic Derivation of the Deviation Parameter $\varepsilon = \gamma/25$}

\textbf{Heuristic derivation}: From the minimal loss principle (Section~\ref{subsec:min_loss}), each fractal coupling channel carries the same scaling exponent $\gamma = \ln 2 / \ln \phi$. \begin{remark}[Lagrangian Formalization of Phase Circulation]
\label{rmk:phase_circulation}
The $25$ coupling modes described above can be explicitly formalized through a phase circulation Lagrangian. The coupling strength between five-state phases is determined by phase differences: adjacent phases (difference $2\pi/5$) have the strongest resonance, with coupling coefficient $\phi^{-1}$; diagonal phases (difference $4\pi/5$) have the strongest anti-phase, with coupling coefficient $\phi^{-2}$. \end{equation}
where subscripts are taken modulo 5. The nearest-neighbor term $\phi^{-1}$ corresponds to resonance-enhancement coupling, and the diagonal term $\phi^{-2}$ corresponds to anti-phase cancellation coupling, fully consistent with the phase coupling description of Section~\ref{subsec:five_states}. This Lagrangian elevates the descriptive phase coupling narrative to a variational formalization, serving as a dynamical complement to the $Z_5$ symmetry-breaking potential (Remark~\ref{rmk:z5_potential}).

\[
w(a) = -1 + \varepsilon \cdot \frac{\Omega_\Lambda a^{-3}}{\Omega_m + \Omega_\Lambda a^{-3}} = -1 + \frac{\gamma}{25}\frac{\Omega_\Lambda a^{-3}}{\Omega_m + \Omega_\Lambda a^{-3}}

This chapter provides a high-compression-ratio scaling law for the three-generation fermion mass spectrum based on the ``Four Locks'' geometric theorems in Appendix~\ref{app:four_locks}.

\subsection{Four-Tree Taxonomy Framework}
Fermions are classified by topological type into four independent fractal growth trees: charged lepton tree, up-type quark tree, down-type quark tree, and neutrino tree. Each tree shares a unified saturation growth law.

Core definitions:
\begin{itemize}
\item Root offset $\delta_f$: the hierarchy offset of the $f$-th family relative to the baseline;
\item Growth exponent $\kappa_f$: the evolution slope of the $f$-th family's coherence degree across generations;
\item Universal criterion: $Q_c \cdot \delta_{\max} = K = \phi^{-4}$ (charge-offset relation).
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Geometric Closed-Form Formula for Three-Generation Fermion Masses}
Based on the Four Locks theorems in Appendix~\ref{app:four_locks}, the three-generation fermion mass spectrum has the following analytical closed form ($n=1,2,3$):
\[
m_n = m_P \cdot 2^{-\nu_e} \cdot C \cdot \exp\left[ \frac{A}{2} \cdot \left( 1 - \frac{1}{1 + D\cdot\phi^{\kappa(n-2)}} \right)^{1/\phi} \right]
\]
