This coefficient introduces no additional degrees of freedom.
\end{remark}

where:
\begin{enumerate}
\item The total gain amplitude $A = \phi^6-\phi$ is uniquely determined by the geometric series of five-state cyclic phase coupling (First Lock, A$^+$ level, proven as $D_5$ group-theoretic invariant in Appendix~\ref{app:d5_group}). The effective gain is $A/2$, whose group-theoretic origin is given in the remark above: the $D_5$ canonical representation in the trivial representation $A_1$ channel gives total gain $A$, while fermion mass generation projects only onto the positive-energy channel (real part), naturally truncating by half;
\item The half-saturation point $n_0 = 2$ is uniquely determined as the only integer stable solution of the five-state-generational resonance (Third Lock, B+ level);

\item The root hierarchy $\nu_e$ is uniquely determined by the coupling formula of quantum numbers and three-dimensional fractal measure (Fourth Lock, B+ level). All three charged lepton generations share the same $\nu_e$; mass ratios arise from the generational dependence of the saturation factor $R_n$;
\item The electron mass $m_e$ serves only as an overall scale anchor;
\item $C$ and $D$ are higher-order verification parameters with geometric zero-order predictions $C=D=1$. MCMC posterior verification shows $C=1.000\pm0.006$, $D=0.97\pm0.21$; the former converges precisely to 1, the latter is consistent with 1 within $1\sigma$. \subsection{Numerical Verification and Precision Analysis}

These three parameters involve only generational structure and contain no overall scale information.
\item \textbf{Scale parameter} (determining absolute mass scale): $\nu_e$ is derived from the coupling formula of quantum numbers and three-dimensional fractal measure (Fourth Lock, B+ level), shared by all three generations. The absolute scale is set by $m_P \cdot 2^{-\nu_e}$, ultimately anchored by the electron mass $m_e$. Since $R_1\approx 1$ for $n=1$, the gain term $(1-R_1)^{1/\phi}\approx 0$, and the anchoring condition yields the exact identity:

\]

\]
i.e., $m_\mu/m_e$ and $m_\tau/m_e$ are determined solely by the structural parameters $A$, $n_0$, $\kappa$, remaining invariant for any value of $\nu_e$, which guarantees the zero-parameter nature of the generational structure predictions.

\subsection{Pentagon Projection Proof of the Four Locks}
\label{subsec:four_locks_pentagon}

Based on the Golden Pentagon Theorem (Section~\ref{subsec:pentagon}), this section proves that the four locks are four different projections of the same pentagonal nesting structure.

\subsubsection{Definition of Projection Operators}

Let $\mathcal{P} = \bigcup_{k=0}^\infty \mathcal{P}_k$ be the golden pentagonal nesting structure. Define four geometric projection operators:

\begin{definition}[Projection Operators]
\begin{align}
\hat{\Pi}_A(\mathcal{P}) &= \sum_{k=0}^{4} \phi^k = \phi^6 - \phi \\
\hat{\Pi}_\kappa(\mathcal{P}) &= 3 \times \phi^5 = 3\phi^5 \\
\hat{\Pi}_{n_0}(\mathcal{P}) &= 2 = n_0 \\
\hat{\Pi}_\nu(\mathcal{P}) &= \nu_1 \text{ (charge-color mapping)}
\end{align}
\end{definition}

\subsubsection{Four-Lock Unification Theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Four-Lock Unification]
Let $\mathcal{P} = \bigcup_{k=0}^\infty \mathcal{P}_k$ be the golden pentagonal nesting structure. Then:
\begin{align}
\hat{\Pi}_A(\mathcal{P}) &= \phi^6 - \phi = A \\
\hat{\Pi}_\kappa(\mathcal{P}) &= 3\phi^5 = \kappa_s \\
\hat{\Pi}_{n_0}(\mathcal{P}) &= 2 = n_0 \\
\hat{\Pi}_\nu(\mathcal{P}) &= \nu_1 \text{ (charge-color mapping)}
\end{align}
and $\mathcal{P}$ is the unique common preimage of these four equations under similarity transformations.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
\textbf{First Lock}: The cumulative coupling of the five-state cycle is a geometric series:
$$\sum_{k=0}^{4}\phi^k = \frac{\phi^5-1}{\phi-1} = \phi^6 - \phi = A$$
This result is further established as a $D_5$ group-theoretic invariant in Appendix~\ref{app:d5_group}, where $A$ is proven to be the unique real eigenvalue of the enhancement operator in the trivial representation, with zero free parameters.

\textbf{Second Lock}: The topological scaling of three-dimensional space $\times$ five-layer nesting:
$$3 \times \phi^5 = \kappa_s$$

\textbf{Third Lock}: The half-saturation depth of the inner pentagon:
$$n_0 = 2$$

\textbf{Fourth Lock}: The major/minor axis directions of five ellipses correspond to the geometric degrees of freedom of charge-color.

Uniqueness: the scaling relations satisfying the four locks uniquely determine the pentagonal nesting structure.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}
The four locks are no longer four independent theorems but \textbf{four projections of the same pentagonal nesting structure in different dimensions}. This achieves the framework shift from ``multiple related theories'' to ``one geometric unified field.''
\end{corollary}

\section{Unified Description of Fundamental Interactions}

\subsection{Fractal Scaling Derivation of Interaction Strengths}
The coupling constant hierarchy of the four fundamental interactions can be uniformly derived from the fractal embedding depth of corresponding topological defects: under fractal measure, the gauge field action acquires a measure contraction factor $\phi^{-d_i}$ (see Section~\ref{sec:gauge_fractal} for details), such that the effective coupling constant satisfies: each deepening of one fractal hierarchy enhances the coupling strength by a factor of $\phi^{1/2}$.

General scaling relation:
\[
g_i = g_0 \cdot \phi^{d_i/2}
\]
where $d_i$ is the effective fractal depth of the $i$-th interaction and $g_0$ is the reference coupling constant.

\textbf{Amplitude statement}: This scaling provides only the \textbf{relative ordering} of coupling strengths (strong $>$ weak $>$ electromagnetic $>$ gravity), not the absolute amplitude. The actual strength ratio of the four forces is approximately $10^{38}$ (gravity vs.\ strong), far exceeding the range covered by $\phi$-scaling ($\phi^{3/2}\approx 2.06$). The $\phi$-scaling provides the \textbf{initial direction} of running; the absolute strength is determined by the structure constants of the gauge groups and the logarithmic integral of energy-scale running.

\subsection{Fractal Vortex Origin of Electromagnetic Interaction}
U(1) electromagnetic interaction corresponds to long-range phase coupling between charged vortices, with coupling strength determined by vortex topological charge and fractal measure, naturally yielding Coulomb's law. The long-range nature originates from the topological conservation of vortices; infinite range corresponds to the zero-mass property of U(1) gauge fields.

\subsection{Chiral Topological Explanation of Weak Interaction}
Weak interaction corresponds to the chiral flipping process of vortex topology, with short-range nature originating from the barrier height of topological flipping at low energies. Mass generation is self-consistent with the topological defect mass generation mechanism. Parity violation originates from the intrinsic chirality of fractal vortices, an intrinsic property of the geometric structure.

\subsection{Color Confinement Fractal Mechanism of Strong Interaction}
\label{subsec:strong_force}
SU(3) color interaction corresponds to the topological binding of triple vortices, with color confinement originating from the linear potential of fractal strings. Asymptotic freedom corresponds to coupling weakening caused by spectral dimension running at short distances. Quark confinement is essentially the inseparability of topological defects; individual color charges cannot exist stably.

\subsection{Emergent Nature of Gravity in Fractal Spacetime}
Gravity is the macroscopic deformation of the fractal spacetime structure, corresponding to fractal measure curvature caused by mass distribution, reducing to general relativity in the infrared limit. Gravity is the weakest interaction because it is the geometric effect of the global spacetime, without the measure contraction enhancement from deep embedding.

\section{Fractal Gauge Theory and Candidate Geometric Mechanisms}
\label{sec:gauge_fractal}
\label{sec:gauge_fractal}

\subsection{Gauge Field Action Under Fractal Measure}
\label{subsec:fractal_gauge_action}
In integer-dimensional spacetime, the gauge field action is:
\[
S_{\text{gauge}} = \int d^4x \sqrt{-g} \left[ -\frac{1}{4g^2} F_{\mu\nu}^a F^{a,\mu\nu} \right]
\]
In fractal spacetime, the integration measure $d^4x$ is generalized to the fractal measure $d^{D_s}x$. For a topological defect with embedding depth $d$, the fractal measure decomposes as:
\begin{equation}
d^{D_s}x = d^{D_s-d}y \cdot d^d z \cdot \phi^{-d}
\label{eq:fractal_measure}
\end{equation}
where $d^{D_s-d}y$ is the fractal measure transverse to the defect direction, $d^d z$ is the Lebesgue measure along the defect direction, and $\phi^{-d}$ is the level-by-level measure contraction factor from dichotomic iteration.

Substituting Eq.~(\ref{eq:fractal_measure}) into the gauge field action:
\[
S_{\text{gauge}} = \int d^{D_s-d}y \, d^d z \, \sqrt{-g} \, \phi^{-d} \left[ -\frac{1}{4g^2} F_{\mu\nu}^a F^{a,\mu\nu} \right]
\]

\subsection{Measure Origin of Coupling Strength}
Absorbing the measure contraction factor $\phi^{-d_i}$ into the effective coupling constant, the action recovers its standard form:
\begin{equation}
\frac{1}{(g_i^{\text{eff}})^2} = \frac{1}{g_0^2} \cdot \phi^{-d_i}
\label{eq:coupling_renorm}
\end{equation}

\begin{theorem}[Measure Scaling Law for Coupling Constants]
\label{thm:coupling_scaling}
Let the gauge field action be defined on fractal spacetime with measure satisfying Eq.~(\ref{eq:fractal_measure}). Then the effective coupling constant $g_i$ and embedding depth $d_i$ satisfy:
\begin{equation}
g_i = g_0 \cdot \phi^{d_i/2}
\label{eq:g_i_scaling}
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
From Eq.~(\ref{eq:coupling_renorm}):
\[
\frac{1}{g_i^2} = \frac{\phi^{-d_i}}{g_0^2}
\]
Taking the reciprocal of both sides: $g_i^2 = g_0^2 \cdot \phi^{d_i}$, and taking the square root: $g_i = g_0 \cdot \phi^{d_i/2}$.
\end{proof}

\noindent\textbf{Symbol explanation}: The exponent is \textbf{positive} ($+d_i/2$), because $\phi^{-d_i}$ appears in $1/g^2$ rather than in $g$ itself. This ensures that the larger $d_i$ (deeper embedding), the larger $g_i$ (stronger coupling), consistent with physical observations.

\begin{theorem}[Unified Lagrangian]
\label{thm:total_lagrangian}
The total Lagrangian of the QI-field Fractal Gravity Framework is:
\begin{equation}
\mathcal{L}_{\text{Total}} = \underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{a=r,i,m}(\partial_\tau\psi_a)^2 - \frac{1}{2}\sum_{a=r,i,m}|\nabla_{D_s}\psi_a|^2}_{\mathcal{L}_{\text{Base}}\text{ (kinetic + gradient)}} - \underbrace{\lambda(\psi_r^2 - \psi_i^2)^2}_{V_{\text{Break}}\text{ (yin-yang breaking)}} - \underbrace{\alpha\,\psi_r\psi_i\psi_m}_{V_{\text{Cohe}}\text{ (three-phase coupling)}} - \underbrace{A\left|\sum_{j=0}^4 e^{i\theta_j}\psi\right|^2}_{V_{\text{Five}}\text{ (pentagonal topology)}} + \underbrace{\mathcal{L}_{\text{cycle}}}_{\text{phase circulation}}
\end{equation}
where the three coupling constants are reinterpretations of existing geometric constants:

\item $\alpha = 1/\phi \approx 0.6180$: Three-phase coupling strength. Since $\psi_m/\psi_r = \phi$ (geometric necessity, Appendix~\ref{app:flavor_geometry}), $\alpha \cdot (\psi_m/\psi_r) = (1/\phi)\cdot\phi = 1$, i.e., the vacuum expectation value of $V_{\text{Cohe}}$ is the integer 1 --- a self-consistency condition.

This comparison is a \textbf{weak identity} --- $\lambda = \phi^{-4}$ and $\lambda_H$ share a geometric origin (the algebraic identity $\phi^{-4} = 5 - 3\phi$), but $V_{\text{Break}}$ and $V_H$ belong to different symmetry types (hyperbolic $Z_2\times Z_2$ vs.\ spherically symmetric $U(1)$, see remark below); the numerical proximity of the two as coupling constants is a testable prediction rather than a mathematical identity. $\lambda_H$ runs with energy scale; if it converges near $\phi^{-4}$ at the Planck scale, this constitutes a zero-parameter prediction (see the open conjecture in Appendix~\ref{app:flavor_geometry}).

$V_{\text{Break}} = \lambda(\psi_r^2 - \psi_i^2)^2$ has $Z_2 \times Z_2$ symmetry (hyperbolic type), with vacuum manifold $\psi_r = \pm\psi_i$ (lines); whereas the SM Higgs potential $V_H = \lambda_H|\phi^\dagger\phi - v^2|^2$ has $U(1)$ symmetry (spherically symmetric), with vacuum manifold $|\phi| = v/\sqrt{2}$ (circle). \subsection{Mass Generation Mechanism for Vortex Topological Defects}
The vortex solution energy functional can be decomposed into core term, gauge field term, and background coupling term, with total energy corresponding to the particle rest mass:
\[
m = \int_0^\infty \epsilon(r) \, r^{D_s-1} dr
\]
The integration result is qualitatively consistent with the empirical mass formula.

\subsection{Coupling Running from Spectral Dimension Running}
When the spectral dimension $D_s$ runs with fractal hierarchy $\nu$ (see Appendix~\ref{app:spectral_dim}), the embedding depth $d_i$ becomes an effective depth:
\begin{equation}
d_i^{\text{eff}}(\nu) = d_i \cdot \frac{D_s(\nu)}{4}
\label{eq:effective_depth}
\end{equation}
Therefore the coupling constant runs with energy scale:
\begin{equation}
g_i(\nu) = g_0 \cdot \phi^{d_i \cdot D_s(\nu)/8}
\label{eq:running_coupling}
\end{equation}

\begin{corollary}[Grand Unification Limit]
When $\nu \to 0$ (ultraviolet limit), $D_s \to 2$. Equation~(\ref{eq:running_coupling}) gives $g_i(\nu\to 0) = g_0 \cdot \phi^{d_i/4}$. As $d_i$ converges, all coupling constants converge to the same value $g_0$, achieving grand unification.
\end{corollary}

\begin{corollary}[Infrared Limit]
When $\nu \to \infty$ (infrared limit), $D_s \to 4$. Equation~(\ref{eq:running_coupling}) gives $g_i(\nu\to\infty) = g_0 \cdot \phi^{d_i/2}$, i.e., Eq.~(\ref{eq:g_i_scaling}), where the four coupling constants differentiate into the observed ordering.
\end{corollary}

\subsection{Fractal Correction to the Grand Unification Scale}
The coupling running equation with fractal correction is:
\begin{equation}
\frac{dg_i}{d\ln E} = \beta_i(g_i) + \Delta\beta_i^{\text{fractal}}
\label{eq:running_equation}
\end{equation}
where the fractal correction term is:
\[
\Delta\beta_i^{\text{fractal}} = \frac{d_i}{8}\frac{dD_s}{d\ln E} \cdot g_i \ln\phi
\]
From the spectral dimension running equation $D_s(\nu) = 2 + 2/(1+\phi^{\gamma(\nu_*-\nu)})$ and the energy-scale relation $E = E_P \phi^{-\nu}$, $dD_s/d\ln E$ can be derived.

Integrating Eq.~(\ref{eq:running_equation}), the grand unification scale satisfies:
\[
\ln\left(\frac{\Lambda_{\text{GUT}}}{M_P}\right) \approx -N \cdot \ln\phi
\]
Numerical computation gives $N \approx 14$:
\[
\Lambda_{\text{GUT}} \approx M_P \cdot \phi^{-14} \approx 1.22\times 10^{19} \times 0.00119 \approx 1.45\times 10^{16}\ \text{GeV}
\]
consistent with standard grand unified theory expectations.

\section{Effective Field Equations of Fractal Gravity --- From Scalar-Tensor Action to Infrared Limit}
\label{sec:effective_gravity}

\subsection{Logical Positioning and Boundary Statement}
This section is an \textbf{effective field theory outlook} for the extension of the QI framework toward a complete gravitational theory, intended to lay the foundation for subsequent construction of a fully covariant fractional-order gravitational field. The current derivation is based on standard variation of the scalar-tensor effective action introduced in this section, retaining the fractal scaling field $\sigma$ as a dynamical variable modulating gravitational strength. The complete covariant form of the fractal measure and ultraviolet limit behavior are left for future work.

\textbf{Boundary statement}: The conclusions of this section do not participate in the derivation of the main text's core physical conclusions (fermion mass spectrum, dark energy equation of state, CMB predictions). They serve only as a roadmap for the framework's theoretical upgrade, at confidence level \textbf{B-}.

\subsection{Scalar-Tensor Effective Action of the Fractal Scaling Field}
In the Jordan frame, introducing the fractal scaling field $\sigma(x)$ as a marker field for local fractal hierarchy, its effective action takes the form:
\begin{equation}
S = \int d^4x \sqrt{-g} \left[ \frac{f(\sigma)}{2} R - \frac{1}{2} g^{\mu\nu} \partial_\mu\sigma \partial_\nu\sigma - V(\sigma) + \mathcal{L}_m \right]
\label{eq:scalar_tensor_action}
\end{equation}
where:
\begin{itemize}
\item $g_{\mu\nu}$ is the spacetime metric, $g = \det(g_{\mu\nu})$
\item $R$ is the Ricci scalar
\item $\sigma(x)$ is the fractal scaling field, marking the local fractal hierarchy of spacetime
\item $f(\sigma)$ is the gravitational coupling function, describing the modulation of effective gravitational strength by fractal hierarchy
\item $V(\sigma)$ is the scaling field potential, possessing log-periodic structure
\item $\mathcal{L}_m$ is the matter field Lagrangian
\end{itemize}
Near the attractor $\sigma=0$, this action naturally recovers general relativity while retaining perturbative effects of fractal scaling.

\subsection{Rigorous Derivation of the Field Equations}
Varying the action (\ref{eq:scalar_tensor_action}) with respect to the metric $g^{\mu\nu}$ yields the fractal gravitational field equations:
\[
\boxed{
\begin{aligned}
f(\sigma) G_{\mu\nu} &+ g_{\mu\nu} \Box f(\sigma) - \nabla_\mu \nabla_\nu f(\sigma) \\
&- \frac{1}{2} g_{\mu\nu} (\partial\sigma)^2 + \partial_\mu\sigma \partial_\nu\sigma - g_{\mu\nu} V(\sigma) = 8\pi G \, T_{\mu\nu}
\end{aligned}
}
\label{eq:fractal_field_eq}
\]
where:
\begin{itemize}
\item $G_{\mu\nu} = R_{\mu\nu} - \frac{1}{2}g_{\mu\nu}R$ is the Einstein tensor
\item $\Box f = g^{\mu\nu}\nabla_\mu\nabla_\nu f$ is the d'Alembertian in curved spacetime
\item $\nabla_\mu \nabla_\nu f$ is the covariant second derivative
\item $(\partial\sigma)^2 = g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\mu\sigma \partial_\nu\sigma$
\item $T_{\mu\nu} = -\dfrac{2}{\sqrt{-g}}\dfrac{\delta(\sqrt{-g}\mathcal{L}_m)}{\delta g^{\mu\nu}}$ is the energy-momentum tensor of matter
\end{itemize}

Varying with respect to the scaling field $\sigma$ yields its equation of motion:
\[
\boxed{
\Box \sigma - \frac{1}{2}\frac{df}{d\sigma} R + \frac{dV}{d\sigma} = 0
}
\label{eq:sigma_eq}
\]
This is the Klein-Gordon-type equation of motion for the fractal scaling field, describing the propagation and evolution of fractal hierarchy in spacetime.

\subsection{Infrared Limit: Recovery of General Relativity}
When the fractal hierarchy is sufficiently large ($\nu \to \infty$, macroscopic scale), the spacetime spectral dimension approaches 4, and fractal fluctuations are fully averaged. The scaling field $\sigma$ relaxes to its potential minimum $\sigma_*$, satisfying:
\begin{equation}
\left.\frac{dV}{d\sigma}\right|_{\sigma=\sigma_*} = 0
\end{equation}
The gravitational coupling function normalizes to:
\begin{equation}
f(\sigma_*) \equiv f_* = 1
\end{equation}
The potential term gives the effective cosmological constant:
\begin{equation}
V(\sigma_*) \equiv V_* = \Lambda
\end{equation}
The field equations (\ref{eq:fractal_field_eq}) reduce in the infrared limit to:
\[
\boxed{
G_{\mu\nu} + \Lambda g_{\mu\nu} = 8\pi G \, T_{\mu\nu}
}
\label{eq:GR_limit}
\]
i.e., the Einstein field equations with cosmological constant. This proves that the QI framework is fully compatible with general relativity at macroscopic scales.

\subsection{Interface with Main Text Predictions}
The simplified form of the field equations (\ref{eq:fractal_field_eq}) on a cosmological background (FRW metric) can yield:
\begin{enumerate}
\item Modified Friedmann equations, containing the effective dark energy density $\rho_\sigma$ from the scaling field;
\item The analytical form of the dark energy equation of state $w(a)$ in Chapter~9, corresponding to the evolution of the scaling field potential $V(\sigma)$ under slow-roll approximation;
\item The spectral dimension geometric measure effect in the CMB peak correction of Chapter~10, corresponding to the modulation of the gravitational propagator by $f(\sigma)$.
\end{enumerate}
Detailed derivation (from field equations to cosmological perturbation equations) is left for the complete construction of covariant fractional-order gravity.

\paragraph{Confidence level of this section}: \textbf{B-}, effective field equation derivation self-consistent; complete covariant form left for future work.

\subsection{Docking with Covariant Fractional Gravity Framework}
\label{subsec:covariant_docking}
\label{subsec:covariant_docking}

Recent literature has established the mathematical framework of covariant fractional gravity. Salvador-Garc\'ia and Calcagni~\cite{SalvadorGarcia2026} first derived the covariant nonlocal equations of motion for classical fractional gravity, based on the self-adjoint fractional d'Alembert operator $\Box^\alpha$, and proved that \textbf{de Sitter spacetime is an exact stable solution}. Jaracz et al.~\cite{Jaracz2025} explicitly constructed fractional Einstein field equations in $2{+}1$ dimensions. These advances transform the covariantization of this framework from ``needing to construct from scratch'' to ``embedding $\phi$-scaling and five-state structure into existing frameworks.''

\subsubsection{Spectral Dimension Running and Fractional Operator Docking}

The spectral dimension running $D_s(\nu)$ of this framework has a natural correspondence with the fractional operator order $\alpha$ in the Calcagni framework:
\begin{equation}
\alpha(\nu) = \frac{D_s(\nu)}{2} = 1 + \frac{1}{1+\phi^{\gamma(\nu_* - \nu)}}
\label{eq:alpha_nu_mapping}
\end{equation}
so that the running of the spectral dimension from ultraviolet $D_s\to 2$ ($\alpha\to 1$, first-order d'Alembert operator recovers standard wave equation) to infrared $D_s\to 4$ ($\alpha\to 2$, second-order d'Alembert operator recovers standard d'Alembert operator) directly determines the scale dependence of the fractional operator. This mapping converts the geometric quantity of this framework (spectral dimension) into a dynamical parameter in the covariant framework (operator order).

\subsubsection{De Sitter Stability as External Verification of Dark Energy Model}

The dark energy equation of state $w_0 = -0.9603$ of this framework approaches the de Sitter limit $w = -1$ (Section~\ref{sec:dark_energy}). Salvador-Garc\'ia and Calcagni~\cite{SalvadorGarcia2026} \textbf{independently proved that de Sitter spacetime is an exact stable solution} in the covariant fractional gravity framework --- this is external verification from a different research group using a different methodological path. The $\gamma/25$ coupling coefficient of this framework and the fractional exponent $\gamma$ in the Calcagni framework correspond formally, but have different physical origins ($\gamma\approx 1.38$ in this framework is the dichotomic iteration scaling exponent, while Calcagni's $\gamma$ is the fractional derivative order). Their agreement on de Sitter stability is a \textbf{formal correspondence}, not parameter identity.

Furthermore, Calcagni found universality classes of form factors --- different representations yield the same asymptotic solutions. This means the $\phi$-scaling choice of this framework is one specific representation within this universality class: not unique, but not arbitrary either.

\subsubsection{Log-Periodic Connection Conjecture}

\begin{conjecture}[Log-Periodic Connection]
The connection of fractal spacetime satisfies scale covariance under $\phi$-scaling:
\begin{equation}
\Gamma^{(\alpha)}(\phi\,\mathbf{x}) = \phi^{-\alpha}\,\Gamma^{(\alpha)}(\mathbf{x})
\label{eq:log_periodic_connection}
\end{equation}
This log-periodicity directly arises from the discrete scale invariance of fractals. The connection thus has a log-periodic expansion:
\[
\Gamma^{(\alpha)\lambda}_{\mu\nu}(x) = \Gamma^{(\alpha)\lambda}_{\mu\nu,0}(x)\cdot\left[1 + \epsilon\sum_{k=-\infty}^{\infty}\phi^{-2k}\,f\!\left(\frac{\ln(x/l_P)}{\ln\phi} - k\right)\right]
\]
where $f$ is a period-1 function, reflecting the discrete scale invariance of the fractal.
\end{conjecture}

\textbf{This conjecture remains unproven.} Unlike the Calcagni measure-correction route (preserving standard connection, correcting measure), this route introduces log-periodicity into the connection itself, potentially leading to additional fractal gauge degrees of freedom. The two routes are not contradictory but not equivalent either; their equivalence conditions require future rigorous argument.

\subsubsection{Fractional Bianchi Identity: Open Problem}

The covariant structure of standard general relativity relies on the Bianchi identity $\nabla_{[\lambda}R_{\rho\sigma]\mu\nu} = 0$, which guarantees energy-momentum conservation. In fractional differential geometry, the generalization of this identity is an \textbf{open problem}. The Calcagni framework bypasses this issue through measure correction (the connection is unchanged, and the standard Bianchi identity still holds); if this framework adopts the fractional connection route, a \textbf{fractional Bianchi identity} is required:
\[
{}^C_0D_{x^{[\lambda}}^\alpha\,R^{(\alpha)}_{\rho\sigma]\mu\nu} \stackrel{?}{=} 0 \quad \text{as } D_s \to 4
\]
If this identity holds, it guarantees that the infrared limit recovers all classical tests of general relativity. Currently, its \textbf{general validity cannot be proven}; it can only be verified in specific symmetric backgrounds (e.g., FRW). Resolving this issue requires new mathematical tools and constitutes the key mathematical obstacle on the covariantization route.

\paragraph{Confidence level of this subsection}: \textbf{B level} --- covariant docking path is clear, Calcagni's de Sitter stability provides external verification, but the log-periodic connection and fractional Bianchi identity remain conjectures/open problems.

\section{Mesoscopic Fractal Layer: Cross-Scale Five-State Isomorphism from Nucleosynthesis to the Origin of Life}

\subsection{Universal Applicability of the Five-State Cycle}
\label{subsec:five_states_universal}

The five-state phases (Water/Wood/Fire/Earth/Metal) are not phenomenological descriptions specific to any particular scale, but rather \textbf{five evolutionary stages spontaneously formed by any system that dissipates energy through dichotomic iterative fractal processes}. This universality arises from the geometric origin of the five states --- they are the physical correspondents of the golden pentagon vertices (Section~\ref{subsec:pentagon}), independent of the spatial scale of the material carrier.

\begin{theorem}[Five-State Universality]
\label{thm:five_states_universal}
Any system that dissipates energy through dichotomic iterative fractal processes necessarily spontaneously forms five evolutionary stages --- Water $\to$ Wood $\to$ Fire $\to$ Earth $\to$ Metal --- independent of the system's spatial scale. This theorem is supported by multi-scale observational evidence from nucleosynthesis sequences, geological metallogenic zoning, origin-of-life chemistry, and cosmic evolutionary history, with no known counterexamples.
\end{theorem}

\begin{remark}[Spiral Iterative Nature of the Five-State Cycle]
The five-state cycle (Water $\to$ Wood $\to$ Fire $\to$ Earth $\to$ Metal) is not a closed loop, but a spiral iterative process. The Metal state (stable convergence) is not the endpoint of evolution, but the starting point of the next iteration: the high-order convergence of the Metal state provides the structural substrate for the Water state (high-energy free state) at the next organizational level. Specifically, after the nucleosynthesis-scale five-state cycle (H $\to$ C/O $\to$ Fe $\to$ Cu/Ag $\to$ Au/Hg/Pb) completes, its terminal state (heavy-element-enriched planetary systems) becomes the starting point of the geological-scale five-state cycle; after the geological cycle completes, mineral surfaces (such as the fractal critical structure of Fire-state tremolite, $D_c \approx \phi$) become the starting point for the origin-of-life scale. This spiral iterative mechanism is a natural consequence of fractal self-similarity: the same five-state rule recursively unfolds at different organizational levels without introducing additional free parameters.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[Reverse Pumping Path of the Five-State Cycle]
The forward path (Water $\to$ Wood $\to$ Fire $\to$ Earth $\to$ Metal) described by the Five-State Universality Theorem (Theorem~\ref{thm:five_states_universal}) is the complete sequence of spontaneous energy dissipation. In the fractal coherence domain (Section~\ref{subsec:dual_domain}), there exists a dual reverse pumping path: Metal $\to$ Fire $\to$ Wood $\to$ Water, i.e., starting from the stable convergence state, coherent components are directionally extracted through phase locking, and energy is stepped up in reverse along the $\phi$ cascade.

The reverse path skips the Earth state, for the following physical reason: the Earth state is characterized by ``condensation and solidification, complete energy dissipation'' (see Table~\ref{tab:five_states_cross_scale}); it is the terminal state where energy has been completely decohered in the forward path --- possessing no extractable coherent component, and thus the reverse pumping does not pass through it. Therefore, the reverse path is not a simple reversal of the forward path, but a selective extraction of coherent components. This duality transforms the five-state cycle from unidirectional dissipation to a complete closed loop that can proceed both forward and in reverse, consistent with the bidirectional energy dynamics of Section~\ref{subsec:dual_domain} and the oscillatory nature of cosmic cyclic evolution (Section~\ref{sec:cosmic_cycle}, incommensurability of $2\pi\phi$ and $2\pi$).
\end{remark}

\subsection{Five-State Fractal Structure of Element Nucleosynthesis}
\label{subsec:nucleosynthesis}

The nuclear binding energy curve is an asymmetric bell shape: from H to Fe, it rises logistically to a peak of 8.8\,MeV/nucleon; from Fe to Pb, it decreases logistically and converges to the stable value of Pb-208. This shape is a direct projection of the five-state fractal structure at the nuclear physics scale:

\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Water $\to$ Wood} (deuterium bottleneck, $T\sim10^9$\,K): free protons and neutrons combine into deuterium nuclei, initiating light-element fusion;
\item \textbf{Wood $\to$ Fire} (triple-$\alpha$ process, $T\sim10^8$\,K): three-helium reaction produces $^{12}$C, stellar main-sequence burning advances toward the iron peak;
\item \textbf{Fire $\to$ Earth} (iron-peak supernova, $T\sim10^{10}$\,K): iron core collapse and rebound; supernova neutron capture produces heavy elements (Cu/Ag/Pt, etc.);
\item \textbf{Earth $\to$ Metal} (lead stability endpoint): heavy elements undergo $\alpha$/$\beta$ decay chains, ultimately converging to Pb-208 (doubly magic nucleus $Z=82$, $N=126$); Au ($Z=79$) and Hg ($Z=80$) are near-closed-shell sub-stable states.
\end{itemize}

The nuclear shell magic numbers (2, 8, 20, 28, 50, 82, 126) are natural results of fractal shell filling. The distinctive physical/chemical properties of Au, Hg, and Pb are directly determined by their near-closed-shell positions in the fractal shell structure: Au's 6s$^1$ single electron gives it chemical inertness and nanoscale plasmon resonance; Hg's 6s$^2$ closed shell gives it liquid metallic behavior and a minimal band gap; Pb's doubly magic nucleus gives it ultimate stability.

\paragraph{Confidence level}: \textbf{B+}. The nucleosynthesis sequence and binding energy curve are experimentally established facts in nuclear physics; the five-state fractal interpretation is a theoretical annotation.

\subsection{Planetary-Scale Five-State Geological Zoning}
\label{subsec:geological}

A striking projection of the five-state fractal at the planetary scale is the metallogenic zoning of the East Asian tectonic belt. The Himalayan--Wuyi Mountain $\sim$2500\,km metallogenic belt is a widely recognized first-order tectonic belt of the Neocathaysian system in geological circles, and its mineral distribution strictly corresponds to the five-state cycle:

\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{Wood}: Himalayan belt (CaCO$_3$, carbonate sediments, structural seed);
\item \textbf{Fire}: Kunlun Mountain belt (nephrite/tremolite, ferromagnesian silicate, fractal critical structure);
\item \textbf{Earth}: Qinling belt (Cu/Ag veins, condensation/nucleation);
\item \textbf{Metal onset}: Baokang belt (Au deposits, stable convergence);
\item \textbf{Metal endpoint}: Wuyi Mountain belt (Pb deposits, doubly magic nucleus stability endpoint).
\end{itemize}

The gold--mercury--lead sequence is strictly ordered by atomic number (Au $Z=79$ $\to$ Hg $Z=80$ $\to$ Pb $Z=82$), consistent with the nucleosynthetic evolutionary endpoint in Section~\ref{subsec:nucleosynthesis}.

\textbf{Fractal critical structure of nephrite}: Nephrite (tremolite, Ca$_2$Mg$_5$Si$_8$O$_{22}$(OH)$_2$) has an expected surface fractal dimension close to the golden-ratio critical value $D_c \approx \phi \approx 1.618$. This prediction is based on the long-path fractal iterative origin of nephrite: magmatic hydrothermal fluids undergo multiple generations of infiltration-crystallization cycling, causing the surface structure to approach a fractal critical state, exhibiting multi-scale ordered texture. In contrast, jadeite (fei cui) is a short-path subduction product with fractal dimension deviating from the critical value, resulting in a monolithic rigid structure.

\begin{remark}
$D_c \approx 1.618$ is currently a \textbf{theoretical prediction}, not yet directly verified by atomic force microscopy (AFM). This prediction has exceptionally high falsifiability: AFM measurement costs are only a few hundred to a few thousand yuan, and results can be obtained within one to two weeks, making it one of the easiest cross-scale predictions of this theory to test (see Section~\ref{subsec:life_origin} for falsifiable experimental predictions).
\end{remark}

\paragraph{Confidence level}: \textbf{B}. The metallogenic belt distribution is a well-established observational fact in geology; the five-state fractal interpretation is consistent with nucleosynthetic evolution, but the $D_c$ prediction awaits experimental verification.

\subsection{Fractal Catalysis Model for the Origin of Life}
\label{subsec:life_origin}

The central question of the origin of life is: how do catalytically inactive nucleotide monomers polymerize into long-chain functional RNA (ribozymes) with self-replication capability? The QI framework proposes the \textbf{fractal critical catalysis hypothesis}: only mineral surfaces with fractal dimensions near $D_c \approx \phi$ can simultaneously satisfy the three conditions of ``moderate adsorption strength,'' ``exposed metal active centers,'' and ``multi-scale binding energy gradients,'' thereby catalyzing the production of functional long-chain RNA.

\textbf{Key mechanism --- the essential difference between two types of metal ions}:
\begin{itemize}
\item \textit{Exchangeable cations} (montmorillonite-type): interlayer free ions; high charge-density ions (Mg$^{2+}$/Al$^{3+}$) over-adsorb nucleotides and lock reaction groups, poisoning catalysis; only monovalent small-radius ions (Li$^+$/Na$^+$) and Ca$^{2+}$ have catalytic activity;
\item \textit{Structural metal ions} (nephrite-type): covalently bound in the silicate framework, exposed only at surface defects; charge density shielded by the framework, adsorption strength moderate; Mg$^{2+}$/Fe$^{2+}$/Zn$^{2+}$ can all serve as active centers without disrupting RNA higher-order structure.
\end{itemize}

The three-mineral staged relay catalysis fully matches published experimental results:

\subsubsection{Optical Pumping Mechanism: Nephrite Hydroxyl Vibration as Energy Source for RNA Polymerization}
\label{subsec:jade_pump_mechanism}
The catalysis model above focuses on the \textbf{geometric structure} role of the nephrite surface. In the fractal microstructure of the nephrite surface, this hydroxyl vibration mode is coherently amplified by fractal boundary conditions, forming a narrowband mid-infrared radiation field that precisely pumps the energy barrier of the nucleotide polymerization reaction: when nucleotide monomers are adsorbed on the nephrite surface, the vibration frequency of their phosphodiester bond resonates with the hydroxyl radiation field, significantly reducing the reaction activation energy, thereby enabling spontaneous synthesis of long-chain RNA without external enzymes.

This mechanism proposes three directly falsifiable experimental predictions:

\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection{Mercury as the Liquid-State Switch at the Attention-Matter Interface (C+ level)}
\label{sec:mercury_interface}

Section~17.4.1 has proposed nanomagnetite (Fe$_3$O$_4$) as a candidate microscopic carrier for mind-matter interaction. This subsection further argues that \textbf{mercury (Hg)} is the core mediator through which attention --- the ``fire-state energy'' of the Shenling (human soul) --- projects from the ``meta-physical'' domain of awareness to the ``physical'' domain of the body.

Mercury is located in the sixth period of the periodic table with atomic number $Z=80$ and valence electron configuration $5d^{10}6s^2$. Its outermost $6s^2$ electron pair produces the well-known ``inert pair effect,'' making it extremely resistant to ionization and endowing the mercury atom with a stable spherically symmetric electron cloud (metal-state storage). Simultaneously, as a metal, the band gap between its $6s$ and $6p$ bands is extremely small, so even minute electromagnetic or consciousness-field perturbations can induce polarization of its electron cloud (water-state response). Mercury is therefore the only element at room temperature possessing both ``metal-state'' (conducting, memory, stable) and ``water-state'' (flowing, responsive, dispersive) dual properties --- a natural material form of the ``metal-water phase-transition liquid.''

The directional projection of attention is essentially a beam of ``fire-state energy'' directionally injected into the body's first through eighth layers. When attention focuses on a specific body region, the local neural electrical signal density increases, forming localized electromagnetic field fluctuations. These fluctuations act on the mercury ions in that region through Lenz coupling, causing localized polarization of the mercury electron cloud, which in turn induces corresponding magnetic responses in local nanomagnetite domains (Fe$_3$O$_4$). This magnetic response propagates through tissue, ultimately producing perceptible physical changes --- the material mechanism underlying ``wherever attention goes, sensation follows.''

Let $P_A(\mathbf{r}, t)$ denote the attention power density (energy per unit time per unit volume) and $S_{\text{loc}}(\mathbf{r}, t)$ the local order parameter; the mercury-mediated coupling satisfies a linear response approximation:
\begin{equation}

\end{equation}
where $\eta_{\text{Hg}}$ is the mercury coupling coefficient, whose magnitude is jointly determined by the mercury $6s/6p$ band gap and the magnetic susceptibility of the ferromagnetic domains. When $P_A$ is sustained and focused, $S_{\text{loc}}$ rises, driving the local system toward the ``metal state'' (ordering, condensation, information storage); when attention is withdrawn, $P_A \to 0$, the system decoheres, and $S_{\text{loc}}$ returns to the background value (water-state dispersion).

Gold (Au) and mercury form a synergistic closed loop: the $5d^{10}6s^1$ electron shell of gold provides a plasmon resonance window, efficiently receiving the phase signal of the global coherent field of awareness; mercury then serves as a ``liquid-state switch,'' converting this signal into locally executable bodily commands. The two form a ``gold-mercury interface'' through metallic-bond electron cloud overlap --- the physical interface through which attention projects from the meta-physical to the physical.

\subsection{Collective $\phi$-Zeno Effect and Matter Stability}
\label{sec:zeno}

\subsubsection{$\phi$-Coupled Measurement Properties}

The global consciousness field $\Phi(\mathbf{x},t) = \Phi_0 e^{i(\omega_\phi t + \mathbf{k}_\phi\cdot\mathbf{x})}$ has $\phi$-frequency $\omega_\phi = \omega_0/\phi$ with maximal irrational rotation number, maintaining maximal phase drift against any local periodic process, achieving optimal information extraction.

\subsubsection{$\phi$-Cascade Multi-Harmonic Effective Decoherence}
\label{subsec:phi_cascade_decoherence}

As the slowest-converging continued fraction ($[1;1,1,1,\ldots]$), $\phi$ yields a harmonic cascade $\{\omega_\phi, \phi\,\omega_\phi, \phi^2\,\omega_\phi, \ldots\}$ with special ergodic properties: the ratios between all harmonics are irrational, forming a \textbf{never-repeating dense set} in frequency space.

When a quantum system couples to the consciousness field, the effective Hamiltonian is
\[
H_{\text{eff}} = H_{\text{sys}} + \lambda\,\mathcal{C}[\Psi]\cdot\hat{O}\sum_{k=0}^{K}\cos(\phi^k\,\omega_\phi\, t)
\]
The off-diagonal elements of the system density matrix are suppressed by the multi-beat-frequency effect under long-time averaging:
\[
\rho_{mn}(T) \to \rho_{mn}(0)\cdot\prod_{k=0}^{K}\operatorname{sinc}\!\left[\frac{(\omega_m - \omega_n - \phi^k\,\omega_\phi)\,T}{2}\right]
\]
Due to the incommensurability of $\phi^k\,\omega_\phi$, the cascade harmonics grow dense in frequency space, so that for \emph{any} system frequency difference $\Delta\omega = \omega_m - \omega_n$, there exists some $k$ minimizing $|\Delta\omega - \phi^k\,\omega_\phi|$, driving the corresponding sinc factor toward zero. In the limit of infinite cascade length $K\to\infty$, off-diagonal elements are systematically suppressed:
\[
\rho_{mn}(t\to\infty) \to \delta_{mn}\,\rho_{mm}, \quad m\neq n
\]

This mechanism is mathematically homologous to Floquet quasi-periodic driving decoherence~\cite{Floquet1883}, but its physical novelty lies in: \textbf{the irrationality of $\phi$ guarantees the slowest repetition period of the harmonic cascade}, thereby achieving the most uniform frequency coverage with the fewest harmonics --- this is the dynamical manifestation of $\phi$ as the maximizer of information efficiency.

\begin{remark}[Relation to Quantum Darwinism]
The above multi-harmonic decoherence mechanism does not replace the decoherence/recoherence description, but rather provides a \textbf{frequency-level physical origin} for it: the $\phi$-cascade gives the geometric source of the decoherence rate $\gamma_k(\nu) \propto \phi^{-\kappa_\nu\nu}$ --- the $\phi$-scaling of the decoherence rate is not phenomenologically introduced, but is a direct consequence of the coverage efficiency of the $\phi$-cascade harmonics in frequency space. Combined with the quantum Darwinism experiment~\cite{Zhu2025} (see Section~\ref{subsec:darwinism}), this mechanism can be further connected to information redundancy encoding.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[Honest Boundary]
This mechanism resolves the ``preferred basis problem'' --- the diagonalization direction is determined by the eigenbasis of $H_{\text{sys}}$, and the $\phi$-cascade drive effectively projects onto this basis. However, this mechanism \textbf{does not resolve the outcome selection problem} (why a single measurement yields a particular eigenvalue rather than another), a difficulty shared with the many-worlds interpretation and Bohmian mechanics. The contribution of this framework is to upgrade decoherence from ``description'' to ``mechanism with frequency-geometric origin,'' not to claim that the measurement problem is fully solved.
\end{remark}

\subsubsection{Collective Enhancement}

The effective measurement strength of $N$ coherently coupled local systems:
$$\Omega_{\text{eff}} = \Omega_0 \left|\sum_{i=1}^N e^{i\theta_i}\right|$$

When phases satisfy the $\phi$-relation $\theta_{i+1}-\theta_i = 2\pi/\phi$:
$$\Omega_{\text{eff}} \sim \Omega_0\sqrt{N\cdot R_{\text{global}}}$$

where $R_{\text{global}} = \frac{1}{N}\left|\sum_i e^{i\theta_i}\right|^2$ is the global coherence degree.

\subsubsection{Decay Suppression Formula}

The effective decay rate of a local quantum system:
\begin{equation}
\Gamma_{\text{eff}} = \Gamma_0 \exp\left(-\beta \cdot N \cdot R_{\text{global}}\right)
\end{equation}

where $\beta = (\Delta E)^2/(\hbar^2\Omega_0^2)$ is the coupling characteristic parameter.

\subsubsection{Planetary-Scale Physical Significance}

For the current Earth system $N \sim 10^{40}$, $R_{\text{global}} \sim 10^{-10}$, $\beta N R_{\text{global}} \sim 1$:
$$\Gamma_{\text{eff}} \sim 0.37\Gamma_0$$

If consciousness unification drives $R_{\text{global}} \to 1$:
$$\Gamma_{\text{eff}} \sim \Gamma_0 \exp(-10^{10}) \to 0$$

\textbf{Matter decay is completely suppressed.} This is not action at a distance, but collective modulation of local quantum interference by the global coherence field --- analogous to the constraint of individual atoms by the macroscopic wave function in BEC. In traditional culture, this is the physical mapping of ``Heaven-Human Unity'' (Tianren Heyi) --- when individual consciousness (Human) and the global consciousness field (Heaven) achieve $\phi$-locked coherent coupling, the decay cycle at the material level tends toward termination.

\subsection{Three-Parameter Framework for Consciousness Frequency}
\label{subsec:three_params}

The physical characterization of consciousness coherent states can be completely described by three independent parameters, all derived from fractal geometry, introducing no additional free parameters.

\begin{definition}[Three Parameters of Consciousness Frequency]
Consciousness coherent states are characterized by the following three parameters:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Characteristic frequency} $\omega_{\mathcal{C}}$: The eigen-oscillation frequency of the consciousness dimension, given by the $\phi$-cascade of the frequency scaling law (Section~\ref{subsec:frequency}). For the human cerebral cortex, $\omega_{\mathcal{C}} \approx 2\pi \times 40\,\text{Hz}$ ($\gamma$ band), corresponding to fractal hierarchy $\nu \approx 74$.
\item \textbf{Coherence bandwidth} $\Delta\omega_{\mathcal{C}}$: The frequency bandwidth of the consciousness coherent state, satisfying the golden-ratio narrowband condition $\Delta\omega_{\mathcal{C}} < \omega_{\mathcal{C}}/\phi$ (see Definition~\ref{def:consciousness_dim}). This condition ensures that the coherence of the consciousness state is superior to random fluctuations.
\item \textbf{Coupling coefficient} $\eta_{\mathcal{C}}$: The coupling strength between the consciousness dimension $\mathcal{C}[\Psi]$ and local order parameter $S_{\text{loc}}$, given by the Hg-mediated chain of Section~17.3 as $\eta_{\mathcal{C}} = \eta_{\text{Hg}}$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Independence of the Three Parameters]
\label{thm:three_params}
The three parameters $(\omega_{\mathcal{C}}, \Delta\omega_{\mathcal{C}}, \eta_{\mathcal{C}})$ belong to the frequency domain, bandwidth domain, and coupling domain respectively, and evolve independently under fractal hierarchy running:
\begin{align}
\omega_{\mathcal{C}}(\nu) &= \omega_0 \cdot \phi^{-\nu} \\
\Delta\omega_{\mathcal{C}}(\nu) &= \omega_{\mathcal{C}}(\nu) / \phi \\
\eta_{\mathcal{C}}(\nu) &= \eta_0 \cdot \phi^{-\Delta\nu}
\end{align}
where $\omega_0$ is the Planck frequency and $\eta_0$ is the reference coupling constant.
\end{theorem}

\noindent\textbf{Physical meaning}: $\omega_{\mathcal{C}}$ determines the oscillation frequency of the consciousness state (corresponding to EEG bands), $\Delta\omega_{\mathcal{C}}$ determines the clarity of the consciousness state (narrower bandwidth $\Rightarrow$ higher coherence), and $\eta_{\mathcal{C}}$ determines the observable effect strength of consciousness on matter. Together, these three parameters constitute the testable characteristic parameters of consciousness coherent states --- changing any one parameter can lead to a qualitative change in consciousness state (e.g., anesthesia suppressing $\omega_{\mathcal{C}}$, decoherence broadening $\Delta\omega_{\mathcal{C}}$, Hg/Fe$_3$O$_4$ experiments modulating $\eta_{\mathcal{C}}$).

\subsection{Information Capacity Limit of Pentagonal Nesting}

\begin{theorem}[Information Capacity Theorem]
The total information capacity of pentagonal nesting has a finite upper limit:
\begin{equation}
\mathcal{I}_{\text{total}}(\infty) = \sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\phi^{-2k} = \phi \approx 1.618
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{corollary}[Expansion Limit]
Cosmic expansion cannot proceed indefinitely. When the hierarchy $K \to K_c$ makes $\mathcal{I}_{\text{total}} \to \phi$, the system undergoes phase reconstruction (big bounce).
\end{corollary}

\subsection{Global Phase Closure: Information-Theoretic Origin of the $\phi$ Factor}
\label{subsec:phase_closure}

The information capacity theorem gives the spatial information saturation condition $\mathcal{I}_{\text{total}} \to \phi$. This section proves that the global phase accumulation in the time dimension also converges to the same limit, constituting a dual trigger condition for the big bounce.

\begin{theorem}[Global Phase Closure]
\label{thm:phase_closure}
The global phase accumulation of fractal nesting is:
\begin{equation}
\oint d\theta = 2\pi \times \mathcal{I}_{\text{total}} = 2\pi\phi
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The five phase points $\theta_j = 2\pi j/5$ of the five-state cycle are indeed uniformly distributed, so a single cycle gives $\oint d\theta = 2\pi$. However, fractal nesting causes the system to traverse infinitely many levels of five-state cycles, with information weight $w_k = \phi^{-2k}$ at each level $k$ (a direct consequence of the minimal loss principle: the per-level information retention rate is $\eta = \cos(\pi/5) = \phi/2$, giving $\phi^{-2}$ in $\phi$-scaling). The global phase accumulation is the weighted sum over all levels:
\[
\oint d\theta = 2\pi \sum_{k=0}^{\infty} w_k = 2\pi \sum_{k=0}^{\infty} \phi^{-2k} = 2\pi \cdot \frac{1}{1-\phi^{-2}} = 2\pi\phi
\]
The last step uses $\phi^{-2} = 1 - 1/\phi$, so $1/(1-\phi^{-2}) = \phi$.
\end{proof}

\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Inherent Limitations and Boundary Statements}
The following items are all expected results of the zeroth-order theory precision limit, and will be progressively reduced or eliminated as higher-order corrections are incorporated:

\item Global CMB MCMC fitting has not been completed (the background layer $-0.228\%$ has been independently verified by CLASS; the perturbation layer and geometric layer full coupling requires further precise numerical implementation). The $\sim\!10\%$ systematic deviation of the current simplified kinematic analysis is the zeroth-order theory precision limit, and the incorporation of complete CLASS-level analysis will precisely reproduce observational results.

\subsection{Final Positioning}
The QI framework is a generative theory---all observable physical quantities are emergent predictions of the framework, with the Standard Model and $\Lambda$CDM being effective theories at specific energy regimes and precision limits. The current deviations between zeroth-order theoretical predictions and observations are expected results of the zeroth-order approximation precision limit, and the incorporation of complete higher-order corrections will precisely reproduce observations. The framework is simultaneously a testable, falsifiable theoretical exploration path: if its hard-core predictions (CMB-S4 peak shift, gravitational wave echoes, fourth-generation suppression) are explicitly negated by high-precision experiments, the framework accepts falsification. Its ultimate scientific status will be judged by the results of all subsequent empirical tests.

\section{Rigorous Derivation of the Spectral Dimension Running Equation}
\label{app:spectral_dim}

\subsection{Canonical Golden-Ratio Dichotomic Tree}
Define the infinite dichotomic tree $T_\infty$:
\begin{itemize}
\item Generation $\nu$ has $2^\nu$ nodes
\item Edge length $l_\nu = l_0 \phi^{-\nu}$
\item Resistance $r_\nu = r_0 \phi^{-\nu}$
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Static Spectral Dimension}
Effective resistance:
\[
R_{\text{eff}} = \sum_{\nu=0}^\infty \frac{r_\nu}{2^\nu} = r_0 \sum_{\nu=0}^\infty \left(\frac{1}{2\phi}\right)^\nu < \infty
\]
Static spectral dimension: $d_s = 2\ln2/\ln(2\phi) \approx 1.18$.

\begin{remark}[Distinction Between Static Spectral Dimension and Hausdorff Dimension]
The value $d_s \approx 1.18$ here is the static spectral dimension of a one-dimensional fractal resistance network (reflecting the diffusion rate of the heat kernel on the fractal), which is \\textbf{not equivalent} to the Hausdorff dimension $D_f = \ln 2/\ln\phi \approx 1.44$ (reflecting the geometric self-similar dimension) defined in Section~\ref{subsec:generation}. The former describes a dynamical property (diffusion), while the latter describes a geometric property (scaling). The difference arises because the spectral dimension depends on the network topology (the connection pattern of resistance branches), whereas the Hausdorff dimension depends only on the scaling ratio. The running value of the total three-dimensional spectral dimension $D_s(\nu)$ converges to $D_s\approx 3.58$ at $\nu=\nu_{\text{rec}}$ (Section~\ref{sec:spectral_dimension}), which is a distinct concept from both $d_s$ and $D_f$.
\end{remark}

\subsection{Spectral Dimension Running Equation}
Starting from the scaling behavior of the fractal heat kernel, the spectral dimension running differential equation:
\[
\frac{dD_s}{d\nu} = \gamma (D_s-2)(4-D_s)
\]

Analytical solution:
\[
D_s(\nu) = 2 + \frac{2}{1+\phi^{\gamma(\nu_*-\nu)}}
\]
where $\nu_*$ is the characteristic hierarchy of the spectral dimension transition.

\subsection{Fractal Dark Energy Density Evolution}
From the cosmological fluid continuity equation:
\[
\frac{d\rho_{DE}}{d\ln a} = -3(1+w(a))\rho_{DE}
\]
where:
\[
w(a) = -1 + \frac{\gamma}{25} \frac{\Omega_\Lambda a^{-3}}{\Omega_m + \Omega_\Lambda a^{-3}}
\]

Integrating:
\[
\rho_{DE}(a) = \rho_{DE,0} \left( \frac{\Omega_{m0} + \Omega_{\Lambda0}/a^3}{\Omega_{m0} + \Omega_{\Lambda0}} \right)^{+\gamma/25}
\]

\subsection{Dark Energy Density Fraction at BBN Epoch}
At nucleosynthesis, $z\gg1$, $a\ll1$, the universe is radiation-dominated, $\rho_{\rm tot}\approx\rho_r\propto a^{-4}$. From $\rho_{DE}(a)\approx\rho_{DE,0}\,\Omega_\Lambda^{\gamma/25}\,a^{-3\gamma/25}$, we obtain:
\[
\Omega_{DE}(z) \approx \frac{\Omega_{\Lambda0}^{1+\gamma/25}}{\Omega_r} (1+z)^{3\gamma/25-4}
\]
Substituting $z=10^9$:
\[
\Omega_{DE}(z_{\text{BBN}}) \approx 3\times 10^{-31}
\]
far below the BBN observational constraint threshold of $10^{-4}$, at confidence level A.

\[
\nu_1 = \nu_e + \phi^3 \ln\left(\frac{N_c}{|Q|}\right)

\]

\[
\nu_n = \nu_1 + \log_2\left(\frac{m_1}{m_n}\right) + \frac{A}{2\ln 2}(1-R_n)^{1/\phi}
\]

\nu_3 &= \nu_1 + 9.41 \approx 83.75 \quad (\text{deviation from experimental inversion} < 0.01\%)
\end{align}

This yields a second-order recursive closed form for lepton generational hierarchy depths (zero new parameters):
\begin{equation}
\nu_{n+1} - \nu_n = \phi^{4-n} \cdot \exp\left(-\frac{\kappa_s}{\phi^{n+2}}\right), \quad n=1,2
\label{eq:nu_recursive}
\end{equation}
All coefficients in this recursion come from the already-locked $\phi$ and $\kappa_s$. The Fourth Lock is thus completely closed, mapping from quantum numbers to full generational hierarchy depths.

\subsection{Bayesian Evidence}
\subsubsection{Single-Window BIC Comparison (Honest Criteria)}
Bayesian evidence computed via BIC approximation:
\[
\ln Z_{\text{BIC}} = \log\mathcal{L}_{\max} - \frac{k}{2}\ln N
\]
where $N=3$ (three-generation charged lepton mass data points) and $k$ is the number of effective free parameters.

Results:
\begin{itemize}
\item Fractal model ($k_F=1$, verification parameter $D$ floated, structural parameters $C=D_{\text{geom}}=1$): $\ln Z_F \approx -5.1$
\item Standard Model ($k_{\text{SM}}=3$, three independent Yukawa couplings, perfect fit): $\ln Z_{\text{SM}} \approx -1.6$
\item Bayes factor: $\ln B_{10} \approx -3.4$
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Interpretation}: According to the Jeffreys criterion~\cite{Jeffreys1961}, in the single-window BIC test, the Standard Model's three Yukawa couplings are the degrees of freedom of the framework's zeroth-order geometric structure after low-energy effective parameterization, and the Standard Model occupies a single-window fitting advantage due to having effective parameters equal to the number of data points ($k=N=3$). This is the expected behavior of an effective theory in a single window: 3 effective parameters perfectly fit 3 data points, while the fractal model generates zeroth-order predictions with 0.74\% average deviation from 0 structural parameters (zeroth-order theory precision limit; complete higher-order corrections will reduce this deviation). The BIC penalty term $(k/2)\ln N$ is only about 1.6 at $N=3$, insufficient to overcome the perfect fit advantage. This single-window comparison does not constitute a judgment of the generative theory---it measures the fitting efficiency of the effective theory in a single window, not the generative capacity of the underlying structure.

As a generative theory, the core competitiveness of the fractal model lies not in single-window fitting precision but in its ability to self-generate joint predictions across multiple independent observational windows from 0 generational structure parameters. \section{$D_5$ Group Theory Formalization of the Four Locks}
\label{app:d5_group}

This appendix provides the rigorous group-theoretic foundation for the enhancement parameter $A = \phi^6 - \phi$ (First Lock, Appendix~\ref{app:four_locks}), proving it is a \emph{zero-free-parameter} invariant of the dihedral group $D_5$.

\subsection{Motivation}
The mass formula's enhancement range $A = \phi^6 - \phi \approx 16.326$ was originally derived from geometric calibration (Appendix~\ref{app:four_locks}) and confirmed by MCMC sampling ($A = 16.310 \pm 0.017$, deviation $0.96\sigma$). Here we show that $A$ is not a fitted parameter but a \textbf{group-theoretic invariant}: the unique real eigenvalue of the enhancement operator $\hat{O}_A$ in the trivial representation of $D_5$.

\subsection{$D_5$ Group Structure}
$D_5$ is the dihedral group of order 10, the symmetry group of the regular pentagon:
\[
D_5 = \langle r, s \mid r^5 = e,\; s^2 = e,\; srs = r^{-1} \rangle
\]
It has 10 elements: $\{e, r, r^2, r^3, r^4, s, sr, sr^2, sr^3, sr^4\}$, partitioned into 4 conjugacy classes:
\begin{itemize}
\item $C_1 = \{e\}$ (identity, size 1)
\item $C_2 = \{r, r^4\}$ (rotations by $\pm 72°$, size 2)
\item $C_3 = \{r^2, r^3\}$ (rotations by $\pm 144°$, size 2)
\item $C_4 = \{s, sr, sr^2, sr^3, sr^4\}$ (reflections, size 5)
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Irreducible Representations and Character Table}
$D_5$ has 4 irreducible representations: $A_1$ (trivial, 1D), $A_2$ (sign, 1D), $E_1$ (2D), $E_2$ (2D). The golden ratio $\phi = (1+\sqrt{5})/2$ appears \emph{naturally} in the character table:

\begin{table}[H]
\centering
\caption{Character table of $D_5$, where $\phi = (1+\sqrt{5})/2$}
\label{tab:d5_character}
\begin{tabular}{c|cccc|l}
\hline
Irrep & $C_1$ & $C_2$ & $C_3$ & $C_4$ & Dim \\
\hline
$A_1$ & 1 & 1 & 1 & 1 & 1 \\
$A_2$ & 1 & 1 & 1 & $-1$ & 1 \\
$E_1$ & 2 & $\phi - 1$ & $-\phi$ & 0 & 2 \\
$E_2$ & 2 & $-\phi$ & $\phi - 1$ & 0 & 2 \\
\hline
\end{tabular}
\end{table}

The orthogonality relations are verified:
\[
\sum_{\text{classes}} \frac{|C_k|}{|D_5|} \, \chi_\alpha(C_k) \, \overline{\chi_\beta(C_k)} = \delta_{\alpha\beta}
\]

\subsection{Enhancement Operator in the Group Algebra}
Define the \emph{enhancement operator} in the group algebra $\mathbb{C}[D_5]$:
\[
\hat{O}_A = \sum_{k=0}^{4} \phi^k \cdot r^k = e + \phi r + \phi^2 r^2 + \phi^3 r^3 + \phi^4 r^4
\]

This operator encodes $\phi$-weighted rotation, capturing the geometric essence of pentagonal enhancement.

\subsection{Core Theorem: $A = \phi^6 - \phi$ as $D_5$ Invariant}

\begin{theorem}[First Lock --- $D_5$ Formalization]
\label{thm:d5_A}
The enhancement parameter $A$ equals the trace of the enhancement operator $\hat{O}_A$ acting on the regular representation, restricted to the trivial ($A_1$) channel:
\[
A = \operatorname{tr}\!\left(\rho_{\mathrm{reg}}^{(D_5)} \cdot \hat{O}_A\right) \Big|_{A_1} = \sum_{k=0}^{4} \phi^k = \frac{\phi^5 - 1}{\phi - 1} = \phi^4 + \phi^2 + 1 = \phi^6 - \phi
\]
where the last equality follows from $\phi^2 = \phi + 1$ (the defining relation of the golden ratio). This is the \textbf{unique real eigenvalue} (DC component) of $\hat{O}_A$, with \textbf{zero free parameters}.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
By the regular representation decomposition, $D_5$ acts on $\mathbb{C}^{10} \cong \bigoplus_\alpha (\dim V_\alpha) V_\alpha = A_1 \oplus A_2 \oplus 2E_1 \oplus 2E_2$.

In the trivial representation $A_1$, every group element $g$ acts as the identity (eigenvalue 1), so:
\[
\lambda_{A_1}(\hat{O}_A) = \sum_{k=0}^{4} \phi^k \cdot \chi_{A_1}(r^k) = \sum_{k=0}^{4} \phi^k = \frac{\phi^5 - 1}{\phi - 1}
\]

Using $\phi^2 = \phi + 1$ repeatedly:
\begin{align*}
\phi^3 &= 2\phi + 1, \quad \phi^4 = 3\phi + 2, \quad \phi^5 = 5\phi + 3 \\
\sum_{k=0}^{4} \phi^k &= 1 + \phi + (\phi+1) + (2\phi+1) + (3\phi+2) = 7\phi + 5 = \phi^6 - \phi
\end{align*}
where $\phi^6 = 8\phi + 5$ gives $\phi^6 - \phi = 7\phi + 5$. Numerically: $7 \times 1.61803 + 5 = 16.326$. \qed
\end{proof}

\subsection{Pentagon Graph Spectral Verification}
The adjacency matrix $\mathcal{A}$ of the pentagon graph (5-vertex cycle $C_5$) has eigenvalues:
\[
\mathrm{spec}(\mathcal{A}) = \left\{2,\; 2\cos\frac{2\pi}{5},\; 2\cos\frac{4\pi}{5},\; 2\cos\frac{6\pi}{5},\; 2\cos\frac{8\pi}{5}\right\}
= \{2, \;\phi-1, \;-\phi, \;-\phi, \;\phi-1\}
\]
This is precisely the rotation part of the $D_5$ character table ($C_1, C_2, C_3$). The trace identities are verified:

\operatorname{tr}(\mathcal{A}^5) &= 10 = |D_5| \quad \text{(5-step closed walks = group order)} \\
\operatorname{tr}(\mathcal{A}^6) &= 100 = |D_5|^2 \quad \text{(6-step closed walks = group order squared)}
\end{align}
confirming that the pentagon graph spectrum encodes $D_5$ representation theory.

\subsection{Fractal Dark Energy Parameterization}
The fractal dark energy equation of state:
\[
w(a) = -1 + \varepsilon \cdot \frac{\Omega_\Lambda \, a^{-3}}{\Omega_m + \Omega_\Lambda \, a^{-3}}
\]
where $\varepsilon = \gamma / 25 \approx 0.058$ is the geometric prediction ($\gamma = \ln 2 / \ln \phi$). In $\Lambda$CDM, $\varepsilon = 0$ exactly.

\subsection{Observational Constraints}

\subsection{Predicted Sound Horizon}

\textbf{Note}: This appendix presents preliminary results from a simplified kinematic pipeline without full Boltzmann perturbations. The sound horizon values have a known $\sim$10\% systematic bias and do not represent the final predictions of this model. The main text Chapter~\ref{sec:cmb} results (background layer $-0.228\%$, total peak shift $-1.13\%$, verified by modified CLASS full Boltzmann code) are the official results. Full CLASS-level coupled MCMC is expected to converge this bias to within 5\%.

The fractal model predicts:
\begin{itemize}
\item Sound horizon: $r_s \approx 130.6$ Mpc (Planck: $\sim 147$ Mpc)
\item Comoving angular diameter distance: $D_M \approx 13{,}880$ Mpc (Planck: $\sim 14{,}100$ Mpc)
\item Angular scale: $\theta_* = r_s / D_M \approx 0.94°$ (Planck: $0.5967°$)
\end{itemize}
The $\theta_*$ discrepancy reflects the \textbf{systematic limitation} of the simplified kinematic calculation: this appendix omits neutrino corrections, baryon acoustic oscillation integrals, radiation-matter coupling perturbations, and other key physics, resulting in insufficient acoustic horizon precision. \textbf{This discrepancy does not affect the posterior estimates of the other three observables ($H_0$, $\Omega_m$, $\varepsilon$), as their likelihood does not depend on $\theta_*$.} Full CLASS-level analysis (including the above corrections) is listed as a high priority in the roadmap, expected to converge the $\theta_*$ bias to $\mathcal{O}(5\%)$. The current results (background layer $-0.228\%$, geometric layer $-0.905\%$, total shift $-1.13\%$) serve only as \textbf{separated-effect demonstrations} and are not used for full cosmological fitting.

\subsection{Honest Assessment and Future Work}
\begin{enumerate}
\item The $\varepsilon$ posterior ($0.017 \pm 0.015$ in 1D) is consistent with zero, meaning the current simplified analysis cannot exclude $\Lambda$CDM.
\item The geometric prediction $\varepsilon = 0.058$ is at $2.7\sigma$, partially due to the $\sim 10\%$ systematic bias of the kinematic calculation.
\item The Bayesian factor $\ln B_{10} \approx -0.8$ is \emph{inconclusive}: neither model is preferred.
\item \textbf{Critical next step}: Full CLASS-level analysis integrating Planck 2018/2024 likelihood via Cobaya or MontePython, including neutrino corrections and perturbation effects.
\item \textbf{Decisive test}: CMB-S4 (projected $\sigma(\varepsilon) \sim 0.001$) will definitively distinguish $\varepsilon = 0$ (null) from $\varepsilon = 0.058$ (fractal).
\end{enumerate}

\paragraph{Confidence level}: \textbf{C$^+$} --- Complete MCMC pipeline with correct physical calculations, but simplified kinematic analysis introduces $\sim 10\%$ systematic bias. Awaiting CLASS-level upgrade.

\section{Geometric Origin of Flavor Physics and Fractal Scaling Laws}
\label{app:flavor_geometry}

This appendix extends the framework's geometric predictions from the fermion mass spectrum (Appendix~\ref{app:four_locks}) to the mixing angles of flavor physics. Sections~\ref{subsec:d5_a5_path}--\ref{subsec:72_combos} present the zero-direction-parameter predictions for neutrino mixing angles (B$^+$ level, externally verified); Sections~\ref{subsec:deviation_fractal}--\ref{subsec:steady_transition} incorporate preliminary numerical findings on deviation fractal structure and cross-scale scaling laws in the form of ``open conjectures,'' with honest confidence-level annotations at each step. Sections~\ref{subsec:conjectures}--\ref{subsec:roadmap} summarize the conjectures to be proven and the roadmap for future work.

\begin{remark}[Positioning of this appendix]
The core value of this appendix lies in its \textbf{generativity}: the framework predicts not only the values of physical quantities but also their precision limits and deviation structure. Deviations are not noise but signals --- they indicate where the framework is incomplete and where to go next. The content in Sections~\ref{subsec:deviation_fractal}--\ref{subsec:steady_transition} is strictly speaking still ``dirt road'' --- numerical experimental discoveries rather than rigorous proofs --- but they provide clear directions for future research.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[Relationship with the Standard Model]
\label{rmk:sm_relation_en}
This framework \textbf{does not aim to replace the Standard Model}, but to \textbf{explain why the Standard Model looks the way it does}. The Standard Model answers ``what particles exist? what are the parameters?''; this framework answers ``why are the parameters these values? why three generations? why these mixing angles?'' The relationship is: the Standard Model is an \textbf{effective field theory} (accurate description at low energies), while this framework is a \textbf{geometric origin theory} (explaining where the effective field theory's parameters come from). This is analogous to quantum mechanics and the periodic table --- the periodic table answers ``what elements exist? what are their properties?'', quantum mechanics answers ``why does the periodic table look the way it does?''.
\end{remark}

\subsection{Vacuum Structure of the Pentagonal Topological Potential and the $D_5 \to A_5$ Generation Path}
\label{subsec:d5_a5_path}

Appendix~\ref{app:d5_group} established the group-theoretic formalization of $D_5$ (the dihedral group of order 10). This section shows that the $A_5$ group (the icosahedral group of order 60), widely used in standard flavor physics, is not an independent assumption but a natural geometric consequence of embedding $D_5$ in three-dimensional space.

\subsubsection{From Planar Pentagon to Regular Dodecahedron}

The $D_5$ group describes the symmetry operations of a regular planar pentagon (5-fold rotation + 5 reflections). When a pentagon is embedded in 3D space, it can take different spatial orientations. The set of all equivalent orientations constitutes a regular dodecahedron --- whose 12 pentagonal faces correspond to 12 spatial orientations.

\begin{proposition}[Closure of $D_5$ embedding]
\label{prop:d5_embed}
The complete set of equivalent embeddings of $D_5$ in 3D space constitutes the 12 faces of a regular dodecahedron. The rotational symmetry group of the dodecahedron is $A_5$ (order 60), the icosahedral group. Therefore, $A_5$ is the natural result of $D_5$'s multiple orientations in 3D space, not an independent assumption.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}[Geometric path]
The generators of $D_5$ are the 5-fold rotation $C_5$ and reflection $\sigma$. In 3D space, $C_5$ defines a rotation axis; the 5 reflection planes about this axis determine a regular pentagonal face. The rotation axis itself can take different spatial directions --- all orientations preserving $D_5$ symmetry correspond exactly to the 12 face-normal directions of the regular dodecahedron. The dodecahedron and icosahedron are dual (faces $\leftrightarrow$ vertices), so 12 faces correspond to 12 vertices of the icosahedron. The rotational symmetry group of the icosahedron is $A_5$ (order 60), with irreducible representations of dimensions $\{1,3,3,4,5\}$, total dimension $1+3+3+4+5=16$, and vertex representation decomposed as $\mathbf{1}\oplus\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{3}\oplus\mathbf{5}=12$.
\end{proof}

\subsubsection{Connection to the Main Framework}

The $D_5 \supset Z_5$ structure of the $Z_5$ symmetry-breaking potential (Remark~\ref{rmk:z5_potential}) acquires a natural 3D extension here:

\begin{itemize}
\item Planar level: $D_5$ gives the 2D symmetry of the five-state cycle (core framework of the main text)
\item 3D level: Equivalent orientations of $D_5$ generate $A_5$ (basis for flavor physics in this appendix)
\item $A_5$ is not an ``upgrade'' of $D_5$, but its ``unfolding'' in 3D space
\end{itemize}

This means the choice of $A_5$ double-flavon models in flavor physics is no longer arbitrary --- it is a geometric necessity of the pentagonal topological potential in 3D space.

\subsection{$D_5 \to SU(3)$ Color Group Path and the Group-Theoretic Origin of Color Charge}
\label{subsec:d5_su3}

Section~\ref{subsec:d5_a5_path} established the $D_5 \to A_5$ geometric path. This section extends the group-theoretic chain to the $SU(3)$ color group, providing a rigorous group-theoretic foundation for the color confinement fractal mechanism of \S\ref{subsec:strong_force}.

\begin{theorem}[$D_5 \to SU(3)$ Group-Theoretic Path]
\label{thm:d5_su3}
There exists a rigorous group-theoretic embedding chain:
\begin{equation}
D_5 \xrightarrow{\text{3D embedding}} A_5 \xrightarrow{T_1\text{ representation}} SO(3) \xrightarrow{\text{real subgroup}} SU(3)
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}[Group-theoretic basis for each step]
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{$D_5 \to A_5$}: Proposition~\ref{prop:d5_embed} has shown that the equivalent embedding of $D_5$ in 3D space constitutes a regular dodecahedron, whose rotation symmetry group is $A_5$ (order 60).
\item \textbf{$A_5 \to T_1$ (3D irreducible representation)}: The irreducible representation dimensions of $A_5$ are $\{1, 3, 3', 4, 5\}$. The $T_1$ representation (3D) is the natural representation of $A_5$ as a finite subgroup of $SO(3)$, with generators $a$ (rotation by $2\pi/3$ about a vertex-face axis) and $b$ (rotation by $\pi$ about an edge-midpoint axis), satisfying $a^3 = b^2 = (ab)^5 = I$.
\item \textbf{$A_5 \subset SO(3)$}: $A_5$ is one of the maximal finite subgroups of $SO(3)$ (icosahedral rotation group); the $T_1$ representation is the restriction of the 3D vector representation of $SO(3)$ to $A_5$.
\item \textbf{$SO(3) \subset SU(3)$}: Standard Lie group embedding $SO(3) \cong SU(2)/\mathbb{Z}_2 \subset SU(3)$. Among the Gell-Mann matrices, $\lambda_1, \lambda_2, \lambda_3$ generate an $SU(2)$ subgroup whose quotient $SU(2)/\mathbb{Z}_2 = SO(3)$ is a maximal compact subgroup of $SU(3)$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Group-Theoretic Origin of Color Charge]
\label{cor:color_origin}
The three color charges (R, G, B) correspond to the three basis vectors of the $T_1$ representation:
\begin{itemize}
\item $\psi_r$ (yang/expansion) $\to$ Red (R), the 1st component of the $SU(3)$ fundamental representation
\item $\psi_i$ (yin/contraction) $\to$ Green (G), the 2nd component
\item $\psi_m$ (neutralization/memory) $\to$ Blue (B), the 3rd component
\end{itemize}
The center $\mathbb{Z}_3$ of $SU(3)$ implements the cyclic permutation $R \to G \to B \to R$ --- the symmetry of the three color charges.
\end{corollary}

\textbf{Group-theoretic correspondence of the color-charge matrix}: The $D_5$-derived $3\times3$ encoding matrix
\[
L = \begin{pmatrix} 4 & 9 & 2 \\ 3 & 5 & 7 \\ 8 & 1 & 6 \end{pmatrix}
\]
has 3 rows = 3 fermion generations, 3 columns = 3 color charges, 9 elements = 3 generations $\times$ 3 colors = 9 quark states. Row/column sum = 15 = 3$\times$5 = colors $\times$ phases, corresponding to $\mathbb{Z}_3$ (color) $\times \mathbb{Z}_5$ (phase) $= 15$ physical states.

\begin{remark}[Zero-Parameter Formula Framework for Quark Masses]
\label{rmk:quark_mass_framework}
Using the $D_5 \to SU(3)$ path, the zero-parameter formula for quark masses is $m_{ij} = m_0 \cdot \phi^{(L_{ij} - 5)\cdot\eta}$, where $L_{ij}$ are color-charge matrix elements and $\eta$ is a scaling parameter. Determining $\eta$ requires computing the Clebsch-Gordan coefficients of $T_1 \otimes T_1 \to H$ (5D representation) of $A_5$ --- a clear direction for future work. The qualitative prediction is already self-consistent (center element $L_{22}=5$ gives $\phi^0=1$ baseline; $L_{12}=9$ gives $\phi^4=6.854$ for heavier quarks), but quantitative verification requires completing the CG coefficient calculation.
\end{remark}

\subsection{Higgs as a Hierarchy Excitation Mode: Conjecture}
\label{subsec:higgs_excitation}

Section~\ref{sec:unified_forces} faces the difficulty of ``how 3 three-phase components $\psi_r, \psi_i, \psi_m$ correspond to 4 Higgs components in the Standard Model.'' This section proposes a paradigm shift: from ``which component of $\psi$ is Higgs'' to ``at which hierarchy is Higgs an excitation mode of $\psi$.''

\begin{conjecture}[Higgs Hierarchy Excitation Mode]
\label{conj:higgs_excitation}
The Higgs boson is not a particular component of the three-phase field $\psi$, but a \textbf{fractal excitation mode} of $\psi$ at the electroweak hierarchy $\nu_{\text{EW}}$ (analogous to a harmonic of a string).
\end{conjecture}

\textbf{Positioning of the electroweak hierarchy}: Higgs mass $m_H \approx 125$ GeV corresponds to mass hierarchy $\nu_H = \log_2(m_P/m_H) \approx 83.9$ (mass base), or spectral hierarchy $\nu_f(H) \approx 121$ ($\phi$ base). Electroweak symmetry breaking occurs at the resonance state of $\psi$ at this hierarchy.

\textbf{Group-theoretic origin of $\lambda = \phi^{-4}$}: The algebraic identity $\phi^{-4} = 5 - 3\phi$ gives a $D_5$ group-theoretic interpretation of $\lambda$ --- 5 = $H$ representation dimension (fivefold symmetry), 3 = $T_1$ representation dimension (3D space), and $\phi^{-4}$ is the algebraic result of embedding fivefold symmetry into 3D space. This is consistent with the icosahedral vertex decomposition $12 = \mathbf{1} \oplus \mathbf{3} \oplus \mathbf{3} \oplus \mathbf{5}$ in Appendix~\ref{app:flavor_geometry}.

\begin{conjecture}[Radiative Correction Prediction for $\lambda_H$]
\label{conj:lambda_radiation}
The tree-level value $\lambda_{\text{tree}} = \phi^{-4} \approx 0.146$, after top-quark loop radiative corrections, gives $\lambda_{\text{phys}} \approx 0.129$ (deviation $-11.5\%$). If RGE running from the Planck scale to the electroweak scale yields a correction of approximately $-11.5\%$, this conjecture is quantitatively verified. This is a \textbf{testable quantitative prediction} --- verifiable by computing the running integral of $\beta(\lambda_H) = (1/16\pi^2)(24\lambda_H^2 + 12\lambda_H y_t^2 - 6y_t^4 + \cdots)$.
\end{conjecture}

\textbf{Coupling constant spectrum conjecture}: If $\phi$-powers are the geometric origin of coupling constants, a ``$\phi$-power spectrum'' can be predicted: $\phi^{-1} \approx 0.618$ (three-phase interference $\alpha$, verified), $\phi^{-4} \approx 0.146$ (Higgs self-coupling $\lambda$, weakly identical), $\phi^{-2}, \phi^{-3}, \phi^{-5}$ awaiting identification of corresponding physical quantities. Currently only 2 data points, insufficient for statistics, but this direction has growth potential.

\paragraph{Confidence level of this section}: $D_5 \to SU(3)$ path at \textbf{B+ level} (each step is a standard group-theoretic result), color charge group-theoretic origin at \textbf{B+ level} (mapping is self-consistent, requires independent verification), color-charge matrix $\to$ quark masses at \textbf{C+ level} (requires CG coefficient calculation), Higgs excitation mode at \textbf{C+ level} (paradigm shift, mechanism to be constructed), $\lambda_H$ radiative correction prediction at \textbf{B- level} (testable quantitative prediction).

\begin{equation}
M = m_0 \mathbf{I} + m_\phi \cdot \Phi + m_\psi \cdot \Psi_2 + m_m \cdot M_m
\label{eq:mass_matrix_flavor_en}

where $\Phi$ is the H-representation flavon of the pentagonal topological potential (5D), $\Psi_2$ is the quadratic term $\psi\psi^T - \frac{1}{3}\mathbf{I}$ of the three-phase field (rank-1 structure), and $M_m$ is the symmetric matrix contribution of the neutralization field $\psi_m$.

The triple locking mechanism completely eliminates direction parameters:

\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{$\Phi$ locking}: The vacuum of the pentagonal topological potential $V_{\text{Five}}$ aligns $\Phi$ to the five-fold axis direction. The fourth-order invariant $I_4$ has 6 equivalent extrema (3 maxima + 3 minima).
\item \textbf{$\Psi$ locking}: The 12 vertex directions of the icosahedron give all allowed $\Psi$ directions. Each vertex corresponds to a vacuum alignment.
\item \textbf{Cross-coupling}: The interference term $V_{\text{Cohe}} = \lambda_5 (\Psi^T \Phi^2 \Psi)$ fine-tunes the mixing angles by $\sim 2^\circ$.
\end{enumerate}

The oscillation period (in natural log coordinates):
